📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期登革病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 总共报告了 138,885 例病例和 14 例死亡。年龄标准化的 发病率 显着增加(AAPC:+30.1%)。高风险 I 类地区占全国负担的 87.1%,尽管其他低风险地区在特定成年男性亚群中表现出加速的 发病率。
  • 我们的研究结果揭示了抗体介导的中和和增强中的基因型特异性变异,强调了抗体反应和 DENV 遗传多样性之间复杂的相互作用。 [10.1128/spectrum.00723-26]。
  • 值得注意的是,与未佐剂的 EDIII mRNA 疫苗 相比,新型 bis-mZDV 的中和抗体滴度提高了 8 倍。更重要的是,在免疫缺陷小鼠中,它可以针对 ZIKV 和 DENV 攻击提供保护,并引发强大的体液和细胞 免疫应答。
  • FPR 第 1 天将严重程度组分开,AUC 0.704 (95% CI 0.579-0.830);重症患者的中位 FPR 是非重症患者的五倍(39.0 vs 7.5;p = 0.001)。针对休克/DSS,FPR 第 1 天的 AUC 为 0.789,严重血小板减少症的 AUC 为 0.727。FLR 第 1 天区分严重肝炎的 AUC 为 0.995(95% CI 0.988-1.000),不高于单独的 ALT(AUC 1.000;DeLong p = 0.23)。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • A total of 138,885 cases and 14 deaths were reported. The age-standardized incidence increased significantly (AAPC: +30.1%).
  • Our findings reveal genotype-specific variations in antibody-mediated neutralization and enhancement, emphasizing the complex interplay between antibody responses and DENV genetic diversity. [10.1128/spectrum.00723-26].
  • Notably, the new bis-mZDV achieved up to an 8-fold enhancement in neutralizing antibody titers compared to the unadjuvanted EDIII mRNA vaccine.
  • FPR Day 1 separated the severity groups with AUC 0.704 (95% CI 0.579-0.830); severe patients showed five-fold higher median FPR than non-severe patients (39.0 vs 7.5; p = 0.001).

  • 登革热病例数量持续上升,2026年前10个月报告病例超过170,000例,与2025年同期相比增加了26%。该疾病现在在许多地区全年发生,包括北境的一些山区省份。 [news-236220c981877370]。
  • 对 2000 年至 2026 年间发表的 宿主 文献的叙述性回顾表明,疾病的严重程度主要是由 宿主 免疫反应驱动的,而不是单独由病毒复制驱动的。 [news-4a53c23ff3696ec9]。
  • 2026年前10个月,登革热病例超过170,000例,报告21例死亡,专家警告该疾病在预防和控制方面的复杂性不断增加。 [news-ae1f7caa166e2f89]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Dengue virus news developments.
  • Dengue fever outbreak; cases and hospitalizations reported. [news-c6600fe3739bbfb1].
  • Dengue fever cases are increasing, with over 170,000 cases reported in the first 10 months of 2026, a 26% rise compared to the same period in 2025.
  • A narrative review of dengue virus literature published between 2000 and 2026 establishes that disease severity is driven primarily by host immune reactions rather than viral replication alone. [news-4a53c23ff3696ec9].
  • Dengue fever cases exceeded 170,000 in the first 10 months of 2026, with 21 deaths, and experts warned about the disease's increasing complexity and challenges in prevention and control. [news-ae1f7caa166e2f89].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Infectious diseases of poverty  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-10  |  DOI: 10.1186/s40249-026-01516-8  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 15, 1
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2005年至2024年中国登革热负担的快速增长:发病率和伤残调整生命年的时空模式和年龄性别差异。

The rapidly growing burden of dengue fever in China from 2005 to 2024: spatiotemporal patterns and age-sex-specific disparities in incidence and disability-adjusted life years.
作者： Si-Han Li, Hong-Rui Zhai, Cai-Hong Xu, Mei-Lan Huang, Jun-Yuan Chen, Qiu-Lan Chen, Yan-Ping Zhang, Xu Caihong
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 登革热 已从散发性输入性感染转变为中国境内已确定的威胁,挑战了其非流行状态的传统观点。了解过去二十年不断变化的流行病学格局对于循证干预规划至关重要。方法 我们分析了 2005 年至 2024 年全国应通报的登革热 监测 数据。使用 Joinpoint 回归评估时间趋势,以估计 监测 的平均年百分比变化 (AAPC),按年龄、性别和省份分层,特别关注广东和云南。通过标准化的省级 发病率 制图来表征空间异质性。根据当地得出的严重程度分布和残疾权重,使用残疾调整生命年(DALY)来量化疾病负担,其中包括生命损失年数和残疾生存年数。结果 累计报告病例138885例,死亡14例。年度病例数从 40 例急剧上升至 24,278 例,主要流行高峰出现在 2014 年、2019 年、2023 年和 2024 年。年龄标准化的 发病率 显着增加(AAPC:+ 30.1%)。25-49岁成年人占所有病例的49.2%,但相对增幅最大的是0-9岁儿童(男性AAPC + 32.7%,女性+ 35.4%)和70岁以上成年人(男性+ 43.3%,女性+ 39.6%)。高风险 I 类地区占全国负担的 87.1%,尽管其他低风险地区在特定成年男性亚群中表现出加速的 发病率。DALY 总数达到 2206.9 人年(年龄标准化率:0.0744/百万),主要由 YLD 驱动,集中在工作年龄人口(79.5%)。结论 随着人口和地理范围的扩大,中国的登革热已进入负担快速升级的阶段。虽然工作年龄人口承受着最大的绝对负担,但儿童和老年人面临着上升最快的风险。这些发现强调了从反应性 暴发 响应过渡到精准公共卫生的紧迫性,整合针对年龄的干预措施、区域分层的 监测 以及 One Health 框架内的血清学监测,为未来的 疫苗 部署提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 已从散发性输入性感染转变为中国境内既定威胁,挑战了其非流行状态的传统观点。了解过去二十年不断变化的流行病学格局对于循证干预规划至关重要。
设计与对象
我们分析了 2005 年至 2024 年的全国应通报登革热 监测 数据。使用 Joinpoint 回归评估时间趋势,以估计 监测 的平均年百分比变化 (AAPC),按年龄、性别和省份分层,特别关注广东和云南。
核心方法
通过标准化的省级 发病率 制图来表征空间异质性。根据当地得出的严重程度分布和残疾权重,使用残疾调整生命年(DALY)来量化疾病负担,其中包括生命损失年数和残疾生存年数。
主要结果
总共报告了 138,885 例病例和 14 例死亡。年龄标准化的 发病率 显着增加(AAPC:+30.1%)。高风险 I 类地区占全国负担的 87.1%,尽管其他低风险地区在特定成年男性亚群中表现出加速的 发病率。
解释与创新
这些发现强调了从反应性 暴发 响应过渡到精准公共卫生的紧迫性,整合针对年龄的干预措施、区域分层的 监测 以及 One Health 框架内的血清学监测,为未来的 疫苗 部署提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
劳动年龄人口承受着最大的绝对负担,而儿童和老年人则面临着上升最快的风险。DALY 总数达到 2206.9 人年(年龄标准化率:0.0744/百万),主要由 YLD 驱动,集中在工作年龄人口(79.5%)。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究设计或数据收集方法的具体限制。证据强度中等,因为研究结果基于 监测 数据和回归分析,这在捕获真实疾病负担方面可能具有固有的局限性。

The rapidly growing burden of dengue fever in China from 2005 to 2024: spatiotemporal patterns and age-sex-specific disparities in incidence and disability-adjusted life years.

Authors: Si-Han Li, Hong-Rui Zhai, Cai-Hong Xu, Mei-Lan Huang, Jun-Yuan Chen, Qiu-Lan Chen, Yan-Ping Zhang, Xu Caihong
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Dengue fever has transitioned from a sporadic imported infection to an established domestic threat in China, challenging the conventional view of its non-endemic status. Understanding its evolving epidemiological landscape over the past two decades is critical for evidence-based intervention planning. Methods We analyzed national notifiable dengue surveillance data from 2005 to 2024. Temporal trends were assessed using Joinpoint regression to estimate average annual percent changes (AAPC) in incidence, stratified by age, sex, and province, with particular focus on Guangdong and Yunnan. Spatial heterogeneity was characterized through standardized provincial incidence mapping. Disease burden was quantified using disability-adjusted life years (DALYs), incorporating years of life lost and years lived with disability, based on locally derived severity distributions and disability weights. Results A total of 138,885 cases and 14 deaths were reported. Annual case counts rose sharply from 40 to 24,278, with major epidemic peaks in 2014, 2019, 2023, and 2024. The age-standardized incidence increased significantly (AAPC: + 30.1%). Adults aged 25–49 years constituted 49.2% of all cases, yet the steepest relative increases occurred among children aged 0–9 years (male AAPC + 32.7%, female + 35.4%) and adults aged ≥ 70 years (male + 43.3%, female + 39.6%). High-risk category I regions accounted for 87.1% of the national burden, though other low-risk regions exhibited accelerating incidence among specific adult male subgroups. Total DALYs reached 2206.9 person-years (age-standardized rate: 0.0744 per million), predominantly driven by YLD and concentrated in the working-age population (79.5%). Conclusions Dengue in China has entered a phase of rapid burden escalation with widening demographic and geographic reach. While the working-age population bears the largest absolute burden, children and older adults face the fastest-rising risk. These findings underscore the urgency of transitioning from reactive outbreak response to precision public health, integrating age-targeted interventions, regionally stratified surveillance, and serological monitoring within a One Health framework to inform future vaccine deployment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever has transitioned from a sporadic imported infection to an established domestic threat in China, challenging the conventional view of its non-endemic status. Understanding its evolving epidemiological landscape over the past two decades is critical for evidence-based intervention planning.
Design and population
We analyzed national notifiable dengue surveillance data from 2005 to 2024. Temporal trends were assessed using Joinpoint regression to estimate average annual percent changes (AAPC) in incidence, stratified by age, sex, and province, with particular focus on Guangdong and Yunnan.
Core methods
Spatial heterogeneity was characterized through standardized provincial incidence mapping. Disease burden was quantified using disability-adjusted life years (DALYs), incorporating years of life lost and years lived with disability, based on locally derived severity distributions and disability weights.
Main results
A total of 138,885 cases and 14 deaths were reported. The age-standardized incidence increased significantly (AAPC: +30.1%). High-risk category I regions accounted for 87.1% of the national burden, though other low-risk regions exhibited accelerating incidence among specific adult male subgroups.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings underscore the urgency of transitioning from reactive outbreak response to precision public health, integrating age-targeted interventions, regionally stratified surveillance, and serological monitoring within a One Health framework to inform future vaccine deployment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The working-age population bears the largest absolute burden, while children and older adults face the fastest-rising risk. Total DALYs reached 2206.9 person-years (age-standardized rate: 0.0744 per million), predominantly driven by YLD and concentrated in the working-age population (79.5%).
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations in the study design or data collection methods. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are based on surveillance data and regression analysis, which may have inherent limitations in capturing true disease burden.

CD8+ T 细胞和体液免疫可能会减轻 抗体依赖性增强 的影响:对登革热 疫苗 设计的影响。

CD8+ T cells and humoral immunity may mitigate the effects of antibody-dependent enhancement: implications for dengue vaccine design.
作者： Riley S. Drake, Hasan Ahmed, Anmol Chandele, Rafi Ahmed, Kaja Murali-Krishna, Rustom Antia, Kaja Murali‐Krishna
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热 疫苗 的发展因 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 而变得复杂化,在这种现象中,抗体反而促进了病毒生长。在 CYD-TDV (Dengvaxia) 疫苗 试验中观察到免疫介导的增强信号,但尚未在 TAK-003 (Qdenga) 试验中报告。为了更好地了解先前的免疫如何影响登革热感染结果,我们开发了一种急性登革热感染动态的 宿主 内机械模型,该模型结合了 ADE 以及体液和 CD8+ T 细胞免疫。模型模拟说明了抗体增强、体液免疫增强和继发感染严重程度增强之间潜在的非常重要的差异。模型结果还表明,CYD-TDV 的接受者可能更有可能经历免疫介导的增强,因为其黄热病病毒骨架以及相应的登革热特异性 CD8+ T 细胞表位的缺乏。该模型为登革热 疫苗 试验报告结果的差异提供了可能的解释,并建议将所有四种血清型的 DENV 主链纳入未来登革热 疫苗 设计中的潜在有前途的方法。重要性 疫苗 通常设计用于引发体液(抗体介导的)免疫,抗体滴度通常用作保护的相关性。由于 CD8+ T 细胞反应更难以测量,因此它们通常不用作保护的相关因素,并且受到的关注相对较少。对于登革热等病毒,抗体可能会增加病毒复制,从而通过 抗体依赖性增强 增加患严重疾病的风险,有必要了解感染期间体液免疫和细胞免疫如何相互作用。我们的模型表明,低抗体水平可以增强病毒复制,但这种效应可以通过体液免疫和 CD8+ T 细胞免疫来减轻。这一结果提供了一个假设来解释 CYD-TDV 和 TAK-003 试验之间观察到的结果差异,并表明评估 CD8+ T 细胞反应以及抗体滴度对于设计和评估未来登革热 疫苗 的安全性和有效性可能很重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 使登革热 疫苗 的发展变得复杂,在这种现象中,抗体反而促进了病毒生长。
设计与对象
为了更好地了解先前的免疫如何影响登革热感染结果,我们开发了一种急性登革热感染动态的 宿主 内机械模型,该模型结合了 ADE 以及体液和 CD8+ T 细胞免疫。
核心方法
我们的模型表明,低抗体水平可以增强病毒复制,但这种效应可以通过体液免疫和 CD8+ T 细胞免疫来减轻。
主要结果
在 CYD-TDV (Dengvaxia) 疫苗 试验中观察到免疫介导的增强信号,但尚未在 TAK-003 (Qdenga) 试验中报告。
解释与创新
模型结果还表明,CYD-TDV 的接受者可能更有可能经历免疫介导的增强,因为其黄热病病毒骨架以及相应的登革热特异性 CD8+ T 细胞表位的缺乏。
科研与公卫意义
该模型为登革热 疫苗 试验报告结果的差异提供了可能的解释,并建议将所有四种血清型的 DENV 主链纳入未来登革热 疫苗 设计中的潜在有前途的方法。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

CD8+ T cells and humoral immunity may mitigate the effects of antibody-dependent enhancement: implications for dengue vaccine design.

Authors: Riley S. Drake, Hasan Ahmed, Anmol Chandele, Rafi Ahmed, Kaja Murali-Krishna, Rustom Antia, Kaja Murali‐Krishna
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue vaccine development is complicated by antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE)—a phenomenon in which antibodies paradoxically boost viral growth. Signals of immune-mediated enhancement were observed in the CYD-TDV (Dengvaxia) vaccine trials, but have not yet been reported for trials of TAK-003 (Qdenga). To better understand how prior immunity influences dengue infection outcomes, we developed a mechanistic within-host model of acute dengue infection dynamics that incorporates ADE as well as humoral and CD8+ T-cell immunity. Model simulations illustrate a potentially very important difference between enhancement by antibody, enhancement by humoral immunity, and enhanced severity of secondary infection. Model results also suggest that recipients of CYD-TDV may have been more likely to experience immune-mediated enhancement because of its yellow fever virus backbone and corresponding lack of dengue-specific CD8+ T-cell epitopes. The model provides a possible explanation for differences in reported outcomes of dengue vaccine trials and suggests incorporating DENV backbones from all four serotypes as a potentially promising way forward in the design of future dengue vaccines. IMPORTANCE Vaccines are typically designed to elicit humoral (antibody-mediated) immunity, and antibody titers are commonly used as correlates of protection. Because CD8+ T-cell responses are more challenging to measure, they are not typically used as correlates of protection and have received comparatively less attention. For viruses such as dengue, where antibodies may increase viral replication and thus the risk of severe disease through antibody-dependent enhancement, it is necessary to understand how humoral and cellular immunity interact during infection. Our model suggests that low antibody levels can enhance viral replication but that this effect can be mitigated by humoral and CD8+ T-cell immunity. This result offers a hypothesis to explain the observed differences in outcomes between the CYD-TDV and TAK-003 trials and suggests that evaluating CD8+ T-cell responses alongside antibody titers may be important for designing and assessing the safety and efficacy of future dengue vaccines.
View research seven-element analysis

Limitations and evidence strength

使用新开发的单轮感染性颗粒组对基因型特异性登革热抗体反应进行全面分析。

Comprehensive profiling of genotype-specific dengue antibody responses using a newly developed single-round infectious particle panel.
作者： Jean Claude Balingit, Ryosuke Suzuki, Mami Matsuda, Dalouny Xayavong, Yuki Takamatsu, Corazon Buerano, Mya Myat Ngwe Tun, Kouichi Morita
摘要中文翻译未标记:中和抗体提供针对 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染的关键保护。然而,在某些条件下,抗体也可以通过 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 增强感染并导致严重疾病。ADE 可以通过外在机制发生,其中抗体调理的病毒粒子与靶细胞上的 Fcγ 受体 (FcγR) 结合,以促进病毒进入增加,而内在机制中,FcγR 介导的摄取触发促进病毒复制的细胞内途径。DENV 广泛的遗传多样性使抗体功能的评估进一步复杂化,因为抗体可能对不同的病毒基因型表现出不同的中和和增强活性。在这里,我们开发了一个单轮感染性颗粒 (SRIP) 组合,代表所有四种 DENV 血清型中的 17 种 DENV 基因型。这个包含基因型的平台能够通过荧光素酶报告定量来评估抗体介导的中和和 FcγR 依赖性感染增强。使用该面板,我们评估了 DENV 感染者、健康志愿者以及黄病毒交叉反应或 DENV 特异性单克隆抗体的抗体反应。我们的研究结果揭示了抗体介导的中和和增强中的基因型特异性变异,强调了抗体反应和 DENV 遗传多样性之间复杂的相互作用。该 SRIP panel 提供了一种强大的方法来分析针对遗传多样性 DENV 毒株的功能性抗体。虽然当前平台主要量化外在 ADE,但其单轮感染格式和基于细胞内复制子的读出为未来研究内在 ADE 机制奠定了基础。总体而言,该平台为研究登革热免疫、评估 疫苗 诱导的抗体反应以及增进我们对抗体介导的保护和疾病增强的理解提供了广泛的应用。重要性:登革病毒 (DENV) 包含四种血清型和大量的遗传多样性,可以影响抗体中和或增强感染的能力。然而,对不同 DENV 基因型的抗体功能进行比较评估仍然具有挑战性,因为病毒遗传背景的差异可能会混淆功能测量。我们开发了代表 17 种 DENV 基因型的单轮感染性颗粒 (SRIP) 组合,其中 prM 和 E 结构蛋白在共同的复制子骨架上有所不同。该设计提供了一个标准化平台,用于直接比较遗传多样性 DENV 毒株中抗体介导的中和作用和 Fcγ 受体依赖性增强作用。通过表征针对广泛基因型的抗体反应,该小组可以揭示功能性抗体活性的差异,而在使用有限数量的代表性菌株时,这些差异可能会被忽视。因此,该平台可用于研究 DENV 遗传多样性如何影响抗体介导的保护和外在 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE),以及评估感染或 疫苗接种 诱导的抗体反应的广度。尽管当前平台主要量化外在 ADE,但其单轮感染形式和定量细胞内报告基因读数为未来在更多生理相关免疫细胞模型中研究内在 ADE 的研究奠定了基础。这种包含基因型的面板为登革热抗体的标准化功能分析提供了一种实用方法,并可能有助于改进对 疫苗 诱导的免疫和跨 DENV 遗传多样性的抗体反应的评估。

问题与背景
中和抗体提供针对 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染的关键保护。然而,在某些条件下,抗体也可以通过 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 增强感染并导致严重疾病。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一个单轮感染性颗粒 (SRIP) 组合,代表所有四种 DENV 血清型中的 17 种 DENV 基因型。该平台能够通过荧光素酶报告定量来评估抗体介导的中和和 FcγR 依赖性感染增强。
核心方法
通过表征针对广泛基因型的抗体反应,该小组可以揭示功能性抗体活性的差异,而在使用有限数量的代表性菌株时,这些差异可能会被忽视。
主要结果
我们的研究结果揭示了抗体介导的中和和增强中的基因型特异性变异,强调了抗体反应和 DENV 遗传多样性之间复杂的相互作用。
解释与创新
该 SRIP panel 提供了一种强大的方法来分析针对遗传多样性 DENV 毒株的功能性抗体。
科研与公卫意义
该平台可用于研究 DENV 遗传多样性如何影响抗体介导的保护和外在 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE),以及评估感染或 疫苗接种 诱导的抗体反应的广度。
局限与证据强度
尽管当前平台主要量化外在 ADE,但其单轮感染形式和定量细胞内报告基因读数为未来在更多生理相关免疫细胞模型中研究内在 ADE 的研究奠定了基础。

Comprehensive profiling of genotype-specific dengue antibody responses using a newly developed single-round infectious particle panel.

Authors: Jean Claude Balingit, Ryosuke Suzuki, Mami Matsuda, Dalouny Xayavong, Yuki Takamatsu, Corazon Buerano, Mya Myat Ngwe Tun, Kouichi Morita
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: Neutralizing antibodies provide critical protection against dengue virus (DENV) infections. However, under certain conditions, antibodies can also enhance infection and contribute to severe disease through antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE). ADE can occur through extrinsic mechanisms, in which antibody-opsonized virions engage with Fcγ receptors (FcγRs) on target cells to facilitate increased viral entry, while intrinsic mechanisms have been proposed in which FcγR-mediated uptake triggers intracellular pathways that promote viral replication. The extensive genetic diversity of DENV further complicates the evaluation of antibody functionality, as antibodies may exhibit distinct neutralizing and enhancing activities against different viral genotypes. Here, we developed a single-round infectious particle (SRIP) panel representing 17 DENV genotypes across all four DENV serotypes. This genotype-inclusive platform enables assessment of antibody-mediated neutralization and FcγR-dependent enhancement of infection through luciferase reporter quantification. Using this panel, we evaluated antibody responses from DENV-infected individuals, healthy volunteers, and flavivirus-cross-reactive or DENV-specific monoclonal antibodies. Our findings reveal genotype-specific variations in antibody-mediated neutralization and enhancement, emphasizing the complex interplay between antibody responses and DENV genetic diversity. This SRIP panel provides a robust approach for profiling functional antibodies against genetically diverse DENV strains. While the current platform primarily quantifies extrinsic ADE, its single-round infection format and intracellular replicon-based readout provide a foundation for future investigations of intrinsic ADE mechanisms. Overall, this platform offers broad applications for studying dengue immunity, evaluating vaccine-induced antibody responses, and advancing our understanding of antibody-mediated protection and disease enhancement. IMPORTANCE: Dengue virus (DENV) comprises four serotypes and substantial genetic diversity, which can influence the ability of antibodies to neutralize or enhance infection. However, comparative assessment of antibody functionality across diverse DENV genotypes remains challenging because differences in viral genetic backgrounds can confound functional measurements. We developed a single-round infectious particle (SRIP) panel representing 17 DENV genotypes in which the prM and E structural proteins are varied on a common replicon backbone. This design provides a standardized platform for directly comparing antibody-mediated neutralization and Fcγ receptor-dependent enhancement across genetically diverse DENV strains. By characterizing antibody responses against a broad range of genotypes, the panel can reveal differences in functional antibody activity that may be overlooked when using a limited number of representative strains. The platform is therefore useful for investigating how DENV genetic diversity influences antibody-mediated protection and extrinsic antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), as well as for evaluating the breadth of antibody responses induced by infection or vaccination. Although the current platform primarily quantifies extrinsic ADE, its single-round infection format and quantitative intracellular reporter readout provide a foundation for future studies investigating intrinsic ADE in more physiologically relevant immune cell models. This genotype-inclusive panel offers a practical approach for standardized functional profiling of dengue antibodies and may facilitate improved evaluation of vaccine-induced immunity and antibody responses across the genetic diversity of DENV.

Neutralizing antibodies provide critical protection against dengue virus (DENV) infections. However, under certain conditions, antibodies can also enhance infection and contribute to severe disease through antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE).

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

通过 CRISPR 工具和药理抑制来靶向 CLK1 可调节先天的 免疫应答 和 登革病毒 复制。

Targeting CLK1 by CRISPR tools and pharmacological inhibition modulates the innate immune response and dengue virus replication.
作者： Berta Pozzi, Agustina Magalí Lucero, Valeria Buggiano, Miguel Angel Pelaez, Cybele Carina García, Anabella Srebrow
摘要中文翻译选择性剪接是已知在病毒感染时发生改变的关键调节机制。这些改变可能来自病毒对剪接机制的直接干扰,也可能来自细胞反应,例如先天免疫的激活。在这里,我们研究了培养细胞中 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染和干扰素 (IFN) 治疗 之间共享的剪接变化,后者作为病毒依赖性先天免疫激活的模型。在常见事件中,我们发现 CLK1 基因非编码 mRNA 亚型的产量增加,从而产生一种磷酸化剪接因子(包括 SR 蛋白和剪接体成分)的激酶。与这一发现一致,IFN 治疗 导致 CLK1 蛋白水平降低。使用具有 CRISPR/dCas9 介导的 CLK1 表达调节的稳定细胞系,我们发现沉默 CLK1 增强了 免疫应答 基因的诱导,而其过度表达则减弱了这种诱导。用药理学抑制剂 TG003 抑制 CLK1 激酶活性,进一步增强 IFN 诱导的基因表达并减少 DENV 复制。总而言之,这些结果确定 CLK1 是 IFN 刺激的基因表达的前病毒负调节因子,并表明其抑制可以增强 抗病毒药物 防御并成为治疗策略的目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索与 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染和干扰素 (IFN) 治疗 相关的剪接变化,因为选择性剪接是病毒感染期间改变的关键调节机制。
设计与对象
该研究在培养细胞中进行,比较了 DENV 感染引起的剪接变化与 IFN 治疗 引起的剪接变化,该变化可作为独立于病毒的先天免疫激活的模型。
核心方法
研究人员使用 CRISPR/dCas9 工具调节 CLK1 表达,并使用药物抑制剂阻断 CLK1 激酶活性,评估对 免疫应答 基因诱导和 DENV 复制的影响。
主要结果
沉默 CLK1 增强了 免疫应答 基因的诱导,而其过度表达则减弱了这种效应。用 TG003 抑制 CLK1 激酶活性进一步增强 IFN 诱导的基因表达并减少 DENV 复制。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,CLK1 作为 IFN 刺激的基因表达的负调节因子,其抑制可能会增强 抗病毒药物 对 登革病毒 的防御。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果强调 CLK1 作为增强 抗病毒药物 防御和改进 治疗 针对 登革病毒 感染的策略的潜在治疗靶点。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于培养细胞,证据基于调节 CLK1 表达和活性,这可能无法完全代表体内条件。证据强度中等。

Targeting CLK1 by CRISPR tools and pharmacological inhibition modulates the innate immune response and dengue virus replication.

Authors: Berta Pozzi, Agustina Magalí Lucero, Valeria Buggiano, Miguel Angel Pelaez, Cybele Carina García, Anabella Srebrow
Original abstract (English)
Alternative splicing is a key regulatory mechanism known to be altered upon viral infection. These alterations can arise both from direct viral interference with the splicing machinery and from cellular responses such as activation of innate immunity. Here, we investigated splicing changes shared between dengue virus (DENV) infection and interferon (IFN) treatment in cultured cells, the latter serving as a model for virus-independent innate immune activation. Among the common events, we identified an increased production of non-coding mRNA isoforms from the CLK1 gene, which gives rise to a kinase that phosphorylates splicing factors including SR proteins and spliceosomal components. Consistent with this finding, IFN treatment led to a reduction in CLK1 protein levels. Using stable cell lines with CRISPR/dCas9-mediated modulation of CLK1 expression, we found that silencing CLK1 enhanced the induction of immune response genes, while its overexpression attenuated it. Inhibition of CLK1 kinase activity with the pharmacological inhibitor TG003 further potentiated IFN-induced gene expression and reduced DENV replication. Altogether, these results identify CLK1 as a proviral negative regulator of IFN-stimulated gene expression and suggest that its inhibition could enhance antiviral defenses and become a target for therapeutic strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore splicing changes associated with dengue virus (DENV) infection and interferon (IFN) treatment, as alternative splicing is a key regulatory mechanism altered during viral infection.
Design and population
The study was conducted in cultured cells, comparing splicing changes induced by DENV infection with those caused by IFN treatment, which serves as a model for virus-independent innate immune activation.
Core methods
The researchers used CRISPR/dCas9 tools to modulate CLK1 expression and pharmacological inhibitors to block CLK1 kinase activity, assessing the impact on immune response gene induction and DENV replication.
Main results
Silencing CLK1 enhanced the induction of immune response genes, while its overexpression attenuated this effect. Inhibition of CLK1 kinase activity with TG003 further potentiated IFN-induced gene expression and reduced DENV replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that CLK1 acts as a negative regulator of IFN-stimulated gene expression, and its inhibition could potentially enhance antiviral defenses against dengue virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight CLK1 as a potential therapeutic target for enhancing antiviral defenses and improving treatment strategies for dengue virus infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to cultured cells, and the evidence is based on modulating CLK1 expression and activity, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate.

孟加拉国登革热 发病率 和 死亡率 的趋势和模式:重新出现的公共卫生问题。

Trends and Patterns of Dengue Incidence and Mortality in Bangladesh: A Re-Emerging Public Health Issue.
作者： Md. Idris Ali
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 2020 年之后,孟加拉国面临历史上一次快速登革热 暴发,这标志着一个重新出现的健康问题。本研究旨在评估2000年至2025年孟加拉国登革热发病率和死亡率的趋势和模式的显着变化。方法采用孟加拉国卫生和家庭福利部管理信息系统2000年至2025年公开的政府数据,对数据进行清洗、编码,并使用Microsoft Excel进行描述性分析。为了评估登革热病例、死亡和病死率 (CFR) 的显着趋势,使用 RStudio 4.6.0 版软件进行了 Mann-Kendall 检验和 Sen 斜率估计器。此外,使用Joinpoint回归软件分析登革热病例和死亡的时间趋势,p值小于0.05被认为具有统计显着性。结果 根据调查结果,2023 年报告了登革热病例和死亡人数最高记录,其中 321,179 例病例和 1705 人死亡,为孟加拉国历史最高记录。2023 年及其他年份,女性和老年人最为脆弱。61 岁以上老年人口的病死率较高,为 2024 年最高。大多数登革热病例和死亡发生在 7 月至 12 月。2000年至2025年,登革热病例和死亡人数显着增加。但病死率逐年没有统计显着性。结论 登革热逐年显着增加,2020年以来致死率更高。需要采取更有效的策略来预防和控制登革热病例的暴露和死亡。该政策建议加强预防登革热暴露的意识计划并加强政府卫生系统的能力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2020 年之后,孟加拉国面临历史上一次快速登革热 暴发,这标志着它成为一个重新出现的健康问题。本研究旨在评估2000年至2025年孟加拉国登革热发病率和死亡率的趋势和模式的显着变化。
设计与对象
该研究使用了孟加拉国卫生和家庭福利部管理信息系统中 2000 年至 2025 年的公开政府数据。数据经过清理、编码,并使用 Microsoft Excel 进行了描述性分析。
核心方法
为了评估登革热病例、死亡和病死率 (CFR) 的显着趋势,使用 RStudio 4.6.0 版软件进行了 Mann-Kendall 检验和 Sen 斜率估计器。此外,使用Joinpoint回归软件分析登革热病例和死亡的时间趋势,p值小于0.05被认为具有统计显着性。
主要结果
调查结果显示,2023 年报告了登革热病例和死亡人数最高记录,其中 321,179 例病例和 1705 人死亡,为孟加拉国历史最高记录。2000年至2025年,登革热病例和死亡人数显着增加。病死率逐年没有统计显着性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,登革热在过去一年中显着增加,并且自 2020 年以来变得更加致命。这项研究为孟加拉国登革热 发病率 和 死亡率 的时间趋势和模式提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要采取更有效的策略来预防和控制登革热病例和死亡的暴露。该政策建议加强预防登革热暴露的意识计划,并加强政府卫生系统的能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于公开的政府数据,这些数据在完整性或准确性方面可能存在局限性。证据是中等的,因为它基于描述性分析和统计测试,没有详细的实验室或临床验证。

Trends and Patterns of Dengue Incidence and Mortality in Bangladesh: A Re-Emerging Public Health Issue.

Authors: Md. Idris Ali
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Bangladesh faced a rapid dengue outbreak in its history after the year 2020 which marked as a re‐emerging health issue. This study aims to assess the significant change of the trends and patterns of dengue incidence and mortality over the year from 2000 to 2025 in Bangladesh. Methods The publicly available government data were used from the Management Information System of the Ministry of Health and Family Welfare, Bangladesh, from 2000 to 2025. The data were cleaned, coded, and a descriptive analysis was performed using the Microsoft Excel. For assessing the significant trend of dengue cases, deaths, and case fatality rate (CFR), the Mann–Kendall test and Sen's slope estimator were conducted using the RStudio version 4.6.0 software. Moreover, Joinpoint regression software was used to analyze the temporal trends of dengue cases and deaths with p value less than 0.05 considered statistically significant. Results According to the findings, the highest dengue record cases and deaths were reported in 2023 where 321,179 cases and 1705 deaths occurred as highest in the history of Bangladesh. Females and the elderly were most vulnerable in 2023 and other years as well. The CFR was high among the older population aged 61+, as highest in 2024. Most of the dengue cases and deaths occurred from July to December. The dengue cases and deaths significantly increased over the year from 2000 to 2025. But the CFR was not statistically significant over the year. Conclusion Dengue significantly increased over the year and has become more fatal since 2020. More effective strategies are needed to prevent and control the exposure of cases and deaths of dengue. The policy suggests to enhancing the awareness programs for preventing dengue exposure and strengthening the health system capacity for government.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Bangladesh faced a rapid dengue outbreak in its history after the year 2020, marking it as a re-emerging health issue. This study aims to assess the significant change of the trends and patterns of dengue incidence and mortality over the year from 2000 to 2025 in Bangladesh.
Design and population
The study used publicly available government data from the Management Information System of the Ministry of Health and Family Welfare, Bangladesh, spanning from 2000 to 2025. The data were cleaned, coded, and a descriptive analysis was performed using Microsoft Excel.
Core methods
For assessing the significant trend of dengue cases, deaths, and case fatality rate (CFR), the Mann–Kendall test and Sen's slope estimator were conducted using the RStudio version 4.6.0 software. Moreover, Joinpoint regression software was used to analyze the temporal trends of dengue cases and deaths with p value less than 0.05 considered statistically significant.
Main results
According to the findings, the highest dengue record cases and deaths were reported in 2023 where 321,179 cases and 1705 deaths occurred as highest in the history of Bangladesh. The dengue cases and deaths significantly increased over the year from 2000 to 2025. The CFR was not statistically significant over the year.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that dengue significantly increased over the year and has become more fatal since 2020. This study provides novel insights into the temporal trends and patterns of dengue incidence and mortality in Bangladesh.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the need for more effective strategies to prevent and control the exposure of cases and deaths of dengue. The policy suggests enhancing awareness programs for preventing dengue exposure and strengthening the health system capacity for government.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on publicly available government data, which may have limitations in completeness or accuracy. The evidence is moderate as it is based on descriptive analysis and statistical tests without detailed laboratory or clinical validation.

针对寨卡病毒的双特异性 mRNA 疫苗 和 登革病毒 可克服 抗体依赖性增强 并在临床前模型中提供保护。

A bispecific mRNA vaccine against zika and dengue viruses to overcome antibody-dependent enhancement and provide protection in preclinical models.
作者： Hui-Ping Shi, Dao-Long Xie, Ying-Han Jiang, Wei-Lun Sun, Pei Sun, Xing-Jie Liang, Xing-Yi Ge, Yuan-Yu Huang
摘要中文翻译寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 是两种广泛传播的黄病毒,对公众健康构成重大威胁。由于它们广泛的共循环和抗原交叉反应性,两种病毒都具有 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 的风险,这种现象使得现有的 疫苗 无法同时防御两种病原体。为了克服这一限制,我们通过整合正交 B 细胞和 T 细胞模块,开发了针对 ZIKV 和 DENV 的双特异性 mRNA 疫苗 (bis-mZDV)。B 细胞模块是通过将 ZIKV EDIII 与自我辅助补体成分 C3d 融合来构建的,以增强中和抗体滴度。T 细胞模块整合了 ZIKV 和 DENV 的保守表位,从而诱导交叉反应性 CD8+ T 细胞,而不引发易发生 ADE 的 B 细胞反应。值得注意的是,与未佐剂的 EDIII mRNA 疫苗 相比,新型 bis-mZDV 的中和抗体滴度提高了 8 倍。更重要的是,在免疫缺陷小鼠中,它可以针对 ZIKV 和 DENV 攻击提供保护,并引发强大的体液和细胞 免疫应答。bis-mZDV 代表了一种有前途的策略,可能适用于与 ADE 相关的共循环病原体和其他病毒。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 是两种广泛传播的黄病毒,对公众健康构成重大威胁。由于它们广泛的共循环和抗原交叉反应性,两种病毒都具有 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 的风险,这种现象使得现有的 疫苗 无法同时防御两种病原体。
设计与对象
为了克服这一限制,我们通过整合正交 B 细胞和 T 细胞模块,开发了针对 ZIKV 和 DENV 的双特异性 mRNA 疫苗 (bis-mZDV)。该研究是在免疫缺陷小鼠中进行的。
核心方法
B 细胞模块是通过将 ZIKV EDIII 与自我辅助补体成分 C3d 融合来构建的,以增强中和抗体滴度。T 细胞模块整合了 ZIKV 和 DENV 的保守表位,从而诱导交叉反应性 CD8+ T 细胞,而不引发易发生 ADE 的 B 细胞反应。
主要结果
值得注意的是,与未佐剂的 EDIII mRNA 疫苗 相比,新型 bis-mZDV 的中和抗体滴度提高了 8 倍。更重要的是,在免疫缺陷小鼠中,它可以针对 ZIKV 和 DENV 攻击提供保护,并引发强大的体液和细胞 免疫应答。
解释与创新
bis-mZDV 代表了一种有前途的策略,可能适用于与 ADE 相关的共循环病原体和其他病毒。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,bis-mZDV 可以针对 ZIKV 和 DENV 提供保护,鉴于这些病毒的共同循环和抗原交叉反应性,这对公共卫生至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在免疫缺陷小鼠中进行的,这可能会限制该研究结果对人类的普遍适用性。证据强度中等。

A bispecific mRNA vaccine against zika and dengue viruses to overcome antibody-dependent enhancement and provide protection in preclinical models.

Authors: Hui-Ping Shi, Dao-Long Xie, Ying-Han Jiang, Wei-Lun Sun, Pei Sun, Xing-Jie Liang, Xing-Yi Ge, Yuan-Yu Huang
Original abstract (English)
Zika virus (ZIKV) and dengue virus (DENV) are two widespread flaviviruses that pose a significant threat to public health. Owing to their extensive co-circulation and antigenic cross-reactivity, both viruses carry the risk of antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), a phenomenon that renders existing vaccines unable to simultaneously protect against both pathogens. To overcome this limitation, we developed a bispecific mRNA vaccine against ZIKV and DENV (bis-mZDV) by integrating orthogonal B-cell and T-cell modules. The B-cell module was constructed by fusing ZIKV EDIII to the self-adjuvanting complement component C3d to enhance neutralizing antibody titers. The T-cell module incorporated conserved epitopes from ZIKV and DENV, thereby inducing cross-reactive CD8+ T cells without eliciting ADE-prone B-cell responses. Notably, the new bis-mZDV achieved up to an 8-fold enhancement in neutralizing antibody titers compared to the unadjuvanted EDIII mRNA vaccine. More importantly, in immunodeficient mice, it conferred protection against both ZIKV and DENV challenges and elicited robust humoral and cellular immune responses. The bis-mZDV represents a promising strategy that may be applicable to co-circulating pathogens and other viruses associated with ADE.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Zika virus (ZIKV) and dengue virus (DENV) are two widespread flaviviruses that pose a significant threat to public health. Owing to their extensive co-circulation and antigenic cross-reactivity, both viruses carry the risk of antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), a phenomenon that renders existing vaccines unable to simultaneously protect against both pathogens.
Design and population
To overcome this limitation, we developed a bispecific mRNA vaccine against ZIKV and DENV (bis-mZDV) by integrating orthogonal B-cell and T-cell modules. The study was performed in immunodeficient mice.
Core methods
The B-cell module was constructed by fusing ZIKV EDIII to the self-adjuvanting complement component C3d to enhance neutralizing antibody titers. The T-cell module incorporated conserved epitopes from ZIKV and DENV, thereby inducing cross-reactive CD8+ T cells without eliciting ADE-prone B-cell responses.
Main results
Notably, the new bis-mZDV achieved up to an 8-fold enhancement in neutralizing antibody titers compared to the unadjuvanted EDIII mRNA vaccine. More importantly, in immunodeficient mice, it conferred protection against both ZIKV and DENV challenges and elicited robust humoral and cellular immune responses.
Interpretation and novelty
The bis-mZDV represents a promising strategy that may be applicable to co-circulating pathogens and other viruses associated with ADE.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the bis-mZDV could provide protection against both ZIKV and DENV, which is crucial for public health given the co-circulation and antigenic cross-reactivity of these viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in immunodeficient mice, which may limit the generalizability of the findings to human populations. The evidence strength is moderate.

具有饱和非线性 发病率 和双时滞的 登革热 模型的分岔分析和最优控制

Bifurcation analysis and optimal control of a Dengue fever model with saturated nonlinear incidence and dual time delays
作者： Akeem O. Yunus, John O. Akanni, Oludolapo A. Olanrewaju, Akeem Yunus, John Olajide Akanni, Oludolapo Akanni Olanrewaju
摘要中文翻译登革热 仍然是一个严峻的公共卫生挑战,数学模型为理解其动态和指导控制工作提供了宝贵的视角。本研究开发并分析了一个七室确定性模型,该模型通过耦合的 传播 途径将人类和蚊子种群联系起来。该模型融合了以前在登革热文献中未结合的几个特征:饱和的 发病率 函数,可捕获 传播 两个方向上的拥挤效应,两个生物学上不同的孵化延迟分别代表病毒在人类和蚊子中度过的时间,恢复后暂时丧失免疫力,以及三部分最优控制框架。在基线条件下,导出并估计控制 暴发 潜力的阈值数量约为 2.37。当该阈值降至或低于 1 时,无病平衡显示为全局渐近稳定;当它超过一时,就会出现一种独特的地方性平衡;对于完整的 DDE 模型,分析地建立了其局部渐近稳定性,并在极限 ODE 情况下证明了全局稳定性(无延迟、无饱和、无免疫减弱)。一个值得注意的发现是,即使阈值低于 1,系统也可以维持地方性 传播,前提是饱和常数满足 α > α † 和逐渐减弱的免疫率 δ h > 0 共同作用,这一结果对根除策略有直接影响。对潜伏延迟的分析表明,超过大约 9.4 天的临界值后,系统就会转变为持续振荡,这为东南亚登革热数据中常见的季节性周期提供了机械解释。敏感性分析强调蚊子 死亡率 是最有影响力的参数。通过分析得出的最佳控制解决方案表明,组合干预策略比单一措施方法更有效。数值模拟验证了所有理论结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 仍然是一个严峻的公共卫生挑战,数学模型为理解其动态和指导控制工作提供了宝贵的视角。
设计与对象
本研究开发并分析了一个七室确定性模型,该模型通过耦合的 传播 途径将人类和蚊子种群联系起来。
核心方法
该模型融合了以前在登革热文献中未结合的几个特征:饱和的 发病率 函数,可捕获 传播 两个方向上的拥挤效应,两个生物学上不同的孵化延迟分别代表病毒在人类和蚊子中度过的时间,恢复后暂时丧失免疫力,以及三部分最优控制框架。
主要结果
在基线条件下,导出并估计控制 暴发 潜力的阈值数量约为 2.37。当该阈值降至或低于 1 时,无病平衡显示为全局渐近稳定;当它超过一时,就会出现一种独特的地方性平衡;对于完整的 DDE 模型,分析地建立了其局部渐近稳定性,并在极限 ODE 情况下证明了全局稳定性(无延迟、无饱和、无免疫减弱)。
解释与创新
一个值得注意的发现是,即使阈值低于 1,系统也可以维持地方性 传播,前提是饱和常数满足 α > α † 和逐渐减弱的免疫率 δ h > 0 共同作用,这一结果对根除策略有直接影响。
科研与公卫意义
对潜伏延迟的分析表明,超过大约 9.4 天的临界值后,系统就会转变为持续振荡,这为东南亚登革热数据中常见的季节性周期提供了机械解释。
局限与证据强度
敏感性分析强调蚊子 死亡率 是最有影响力的参数。数值模拟验证了所有理论结果。

Bifurcation analysis and optimal control of a Dengue fever model with saturated nonlinear incidence and dual time delays

Authors: Akeem O. Yunus, John O. Akanni, Oludolapo A. Olanrewaju, Akeem Yunus, John Olajide Akanni, Oludolapo Akanni Olanrewaju
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever remains a serious public health challenge, and mathematical models offer a valuable lens for understanding its dynamics and guiding control efforts. This study develops and analyses a seven-compartment deterministic model that links human and mosquito populations through coupled transmission pathways. The model incorporates several features not previously combined in the dengue literature: a saturated incidence function that captures crowding effects in both transmission directions, two biologically distinct incubation delays representing the time the virus spends in humans and in mosquitoes separately, gradual loss of temporary immunity after recovery, and a three-component optimal control framework. The threshold quantity governing outbreak potential is derived and estimated at approximately 2.37 under baseline conditions. When this threshold falls to or below one, the disease-free equilibrium is shown to be globally asymptotically stable; when it exceeds one, a unique endemic equilibrium emerges; its local asymptotic stability is established analytically for the full DDE model, and global stability is proved for the limiting ODE case (no delays, no saturation, no waning immunity). A notable finding is that the system can sustain endemic transmission even when the threshold is driven below one, provided that the saturation constant satisfies α > α † and the waning immunity rate δ h > 0 act together—a result with direct implications for eradication strategies. Analysis of the incubation delay reveals that beyond a critical value of roughly 9.4 days, the system transitions into sustained oscillations, offering a mechanistic explanation for the seasonal cycles commonly observed in Southeast Asian dengue data. Sensitivity analysis highlights mosquito mortality as the most influential parameter. Optimal control solutions, derived analytically, point to combined intervention strategies as substantially more effective than single-measure approaches. Numerical simulations validate all theoretical results.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever remains a serious public health challenge, and mathematical models offer a valuable lens for understanding its dynamics and guiding control efforts.

登革病毒-1谱系之间的变异影响感染和疫苗接种引起的抗体反应的中和广度

Variation among dengue virus-1 lineages affects the neutralization breadth of antibody responses elicited by infection and vaccination
作者： Tyshawn Ferrell, Nayan Mallubhotla, Diana Rojas-Gallardo, Daniel O. Espinoza, Elizabeth D. Somsen, Austin G. Leach, Beatriz Giraldo, Diego López, Paula Escobar, Erika Vanessa Jiménez-Posada
摘要中文翻译摘要 疫苗 (DENV) 血清型内的遗传多样性可能影响 疫苗 诱导免疫的广度和持久性。我们开发了一组 DENV-1 报告病毒颗粒,涵盖约 74% 的已知全球包膜蛋白多样性,并使用来自四价 疫苗 受体、实验性 DENV-1 攻击参与者和单型自然感染个体的血清评估了中和作用。中和作用在 DENV-1 谱系之间存在很大差异,并且因暴露史而异。尽管总体抗体滴度较高,但 疫苗接种 后的几种变体显示中和作用减少。随后的 DENV-1 攻击扩大了反应范围,在 180 天内反应仍然较高,而未接种疫苗的个体的攻击则随着时间的推移产生较低的效力和更多谱系特异性的降低。自然感染在整个面板上产生了相对均匀的中和作用。我们使用抗原制图来识别与 疫苗 和攻击血清中抗原距离增加和中和减少相关的特定包膜氨基酸位置。这些发现证明了血清型内存在显着的抗原变异,并支持使用不同的谱系来评估 DENV 疫苗 诱导的免疫。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 登革病毒-1 (DENV-1) 谱系内的遗传变异如何影响感染和 疫苗接种 引起的抗体反应的广度和持久性。背景强调 DENV 遗传多样性可能会影响 疫苗 诱导的免疫。
设计与对象
该研究使用四价 疫苗 受体、实验性 DENV-1 攻击参与者和单型自然感染个体的血清评估了中和反应。开发了一组 DENV-1 报告病毒颗粒,涵盖约 74% 的已知全球包膜蛋白多样性。
核心方法
采用抗原制图来识别与 疫苗 和攻击血清中抗原距离增加和中和减少相关的特定包膜氨基酸位置。通过评估对 DENV-1 小组的反应来评估中和作用。
主要结果
尽管总体抗体滴度较高,但在 疫苗接种 后,一些 DENV-1 变体表现出中和作用减少。随后的 DENV-1 攻击扩大了反应范围,在 180 天内反应仍然较高,而未接种疫苗的个体的攻击则随着时间的推移产生较低的效力和更多谱系特异性的降低。
解释与创新
研究结果证明了显着的血清型内抗原变异,并表明应使用不同的 DENV-1 谱系来评估 疫苗 诱导的免疫。这凸显了谱系多样性在评估 免疫应答 中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于改进 DENV 疫苗 的设计和了解 免疫应答 针对不同病毒谱系的有效性具有重要意义。他们还支持 疫苗 开发中需要更广泛的抗原覆盖范围。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖特定人群的血清和报告病毒颗粒的使用而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为研究结果是基于观察到的中和模式,没有直接的因果证据。

Variation among dengue virus-1 lineages affects the neutralization breadth of antibody responses elicited by infection and vaccination

Authors: Tyshawn Ferrell, Nayan Mallubhotla, Diana Rojas-Gallardo, Daniel O. Espinoza, Elizabeth D. Somsen, Austin G. Leach, Beatriz Giraldo, Diego López, Paula Escobar, Erika Vanessa Jiménez-Posada
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue virus (DENV) genetic diversity within serotypes may influence the breadth and durability of vaccine-induced immunity. We developed a panel of DENV-1 reporter virus particles encompassing ~ 74% of known global envelope protein diversity and evaluated neutralization using sera from tetravalent vaccine recipients, experimental DENV-1 challenge participants, and individuals with monotypic natural infection. Neutralization varied substantially among DENV-1 lineages and differed by exposure history. Several variants showed reduced neutralization following vaccination despite high overall antibody titers. Subsequent DENV-1 challenge broadened responses, which remained elevated through 180 days, whereas challenge of unvaccinated individuals produced lower potency and more lineage-specific decreases over time. Natural infection produced comparatively uniform neutralization across the panel. We used antigenic cartography to identify specific envelope amino acid positions associated with increased antigenic distance and reduced neutralization across vaccine and challenge sera. These findings demonstrate substantial within-serotype antigenic variation and support using diverse lineages to assess DENV vaccine-induced immunity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate how genetic variation within dengue virus-1 (DENV-1) lineages influences the breadth and durability of antibody responses elicited by infection and vaccination. The background highlights that DENV genetic diversity may impact vaccine-induced immunity.
Design and population
The study evaluated neutralization responses using sera from tetravalent vaccine recipients, experimental DENV-1 challenge participants, and individuals with monotypic natural infection. A panel of DENV-1 reporter virus particles was developed to encompass ~74% of known global envelope protein diversity.
Core methods
Antigenic cartography was employed to identify specific envelope amino acid positions linked to increased antigenic distance and reduced neutralization across vaccine and challenge sera. Neutralization was assessed by evaluating responses to the DENV-1 panel.
Main results
Several DENV-1 variants exhibited reduced neutralization following vaccination, despite high overall antibody titers. Subsequent DENV-1 challenge broadened responses, which remained elevated through 180 days, whereas challenge of unvaccinated individuals produced lower potency and more lineage-specific decreases over time.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate significant within-serotype antigenic variation and suggest that diverse DENV-1 lineages should be used to evaluate vaccine-induced immunity. This highlights the importance of lineage diversity in assessing immune responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results have implications for improving the design of DENV vaccines and understanding the effectiveness of immune responses against different viral lineages. They also support the need for broader antigenic coverage in vaccine development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on sera from specific populations and the use of reporter virus particles. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on observed neutralization patterns without direct causal evidence.

住院登革热成人血清铁蛋白动力学和铁蛋白衍生比率:一项前瞻性系列生物标志物研究。

Serum ferritin kinetics and ferritin-derived ratios in hospitalised adults with dengue: A prospective serial biomarker study.
作者： Vinod Dandge, Mineshkumar Rajendrabhai Chaudhary
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热在印度造成重大发病率和 死亡率,早期识别进展为登革热休克综合征或严重肝炎的患者的需求仍然未得到满足。血清铁蛋白反映了巨噬细胞的激活,但其系列动力学和铁蛋白衍生的综合指数尚未在印度成年人群中得到严格评估。我们表征了系列铁蛋白动力学并评估了两个铁蛋白衍生比率:铁蛋白与血小板比率 (FPR) 和铁蛋白/ALT 比率 (FLR)。方法:这项前瞻性观察性研究在苏拉特 SMIMER 招募了 100 名 2009 年 WHO 确诊的登革热成人患者(2020 年 6 月至 2021 年 11 月)。在医院第 1、3 和 5 天通过夹心 ELISA 测量血清铁蛋白,并按患病日期进行分析;血小板计数和转氨酶是住院期间记录的最低和最高值,如果在入院和出院之间的任何时间出现,则记录结果。严重程度分为非严重 (n = 68) 或严重 (n = 32)。非循环终点——休克/DSS (n = 22)、严重血小板减少症 <20,000/μL (n = 28) 和严重肝炎 AST/ALT ≥ 1,000 IU/L (n = 18)——是诊断准确性的主要结局。FPR 和 FLR 是预先指定的综合指数,并通过配对 DeLong 检验与其成分检验进行比较。主要发现:铁蛋白在住院期间表现出上升-峰值-下降轨迹,在第 3 天达到峰值(平均值 955 ± 177 ng/ml;Friedman p < 10-16);按患病日期,中位数从 700 ng/ml(患病天数≤3 天)升至 950 ng/ml(第 6-7 天),然后趋于稳定。FPR 第 1 天将严重程度组分开,AUC 0.704 (95% CI 0.579-0.830);重症患者的中位 FPR 是非重症患者的五倍(39.0 vs 7.5;p = 0.001)。针对休克/DSS,FPR 第 1 天的 AUC 0.801(95% CI 0.688-0.898),不高于单独的血小板计数(AUC 0.784;DeLong p = 0.41)。FLR 第 1 天区分严重肝炎的 AUC 为 0.995(95% CI 0.988-1.000),不高于单独的 ALT(AUC 1.000;DeLong p = 0.23),该变量定义了这一结果。针对非循环结果,第 1 天铁蛋白区分休克/DSS(AUC 0.789)和严重血小板减少症(AUC 0.727)。铁蛋白-血小板负相关与人口统计无关(部分 Spearman rho=-0.392;p < 0.001)。与单独的血小板计数相比,铁蛋白没有提供任何增量益处(NRI = 0.143;IDI = 0.000)。结论/意义:血清铁蛋白从入院第 3 天开始升高,休克和严重血小板减少症患者的血清铁蛋白升高。铁蛋白与血小板比率和铁蛋白/ALT 比率成本较低,但两者的区分效果都比其包含的常规测试(血小板计数或 ALT)更好。由于血小板计数和转氨酶作为入院时的最低值和最高值进入比率,并且在整个住院期间记录结果,因此这两个比率都是疾病严重程度的标志,而不是后来休克或严重肝炎的预测因素。需要进行前瞻性多中心研究,包括实验室值和入院后的结果,以测试任何预测作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热在印度造成重大发病率和 死亡率,早期识别进展为登革热休克综合征或严重肝炎的患者的需求仍然未得到满足。我们表征了系列铁蛋白动力学并评估了两个铁蛋白衍生比率:铁蛋白与血小板比率 (FPR) 和铁蛋白/ALT 比率 (FLR)。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性观察性研究在苏拉特 SMIMER 招募了 100 名 WHO 2009 年确诊的登革热成人患者(2020 年 6 月至 2021 年 11 月)。严重程度分为非严重 (n = 68) 或严重 (n = 32)。
核心方法
在医院第 1、3 和 5 天通过夹心 ELISA 测量血清铁蛋白,并按患病日期进行分析;血小板计数和转氨酶是住院期间记录的最低和最高值,如果在入院和出院之间的任何时间出现,则记录结果。铁蛋白与血小板比率和铁蛋白/ALT 比率成本较低,但两者的区分效果都比其包含的常规测试(血小板计数或 ALT)更好。
主要结果
FPR 第 1 天将严重程度组分开,AUC 0.704 (95% CI 0.579-0.830);重症患者的中位 FPR 是非重症患者的五倍(39.0 vs 7.5;p = 0.001)。针对休克/DSS,FPR 第 1 天的 AUC 为 0.789,严重血小板减少症的 AUC 为 0.727。FLR 第 1 天区分严重肝炎的 AUC 为 0.995(95% CI 0.988-1.000),不高于单独的 ALT(AUC 1.000;DeLong p = 0.23)。
解释与创新
血清铁蛋白从入院第3天开始上升,休克和严重血小板减少症患者的血清铁蛋白更高。铁蛋白-血小板负相关与人口统计无关(部分 Spearman rho=-0.392;p < 0.001)。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,血清铁蛋白可能是监测住院登革热成人疾病严重程度的有用生物标志物。然而,这些比率并没有比现有的常规测试有显着改善。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其观察设计和缺乏比率预测价值而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为该研究提供了描述性发现,但没有建立因果关系或预测效用。

Serum ferritin kinetics and ferritin-derived ratios in hospitalised adults with dengue: A prospective serial biomarker study.

Authors: Vinod Dandge, Mineshkumar Rajendrabhai Chaudhary
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue causes major morbidity and mortality across India, where early identification of patients progressing to dengue shock syndrome or severe hepatitis remains an unmet need. Serum ferritin reflects macrophage activation, but its serial kinetics and ferritin-derived composite indices have not been rigorously evaluated in adult Indian populations. We characterised serial ferritin kinetics and evaluated two ferritin-derived ratios: the Ferritin-to-Platelet Ratio (FPR) and the Ferritin/ALT Ratio (FLR). METHODOLOGY: This prospective observational study enrolled 100 adult WHO 2009-confirmed dengue patients at SMIMER, Surat (June 2020-November 2021). Serum ferritin was measured by sandwich ELISA on hospital Days 1, 3 and 5 and was also analysed by day of illness; platelet count and transaminases were the lowest and highest values recorded during the stay, and outcomes were recorded if present at any time between admission and discharge. Severity was classified as non-severe (n = 68) or severe (n = 32). Non-circular endpoints-shock/DSS (n = 22), severe thrombocytopenia <20,000/μL (n = 28), and severe hepatitis AST/ALT ≥ 1,000 IU/L (n = 18)-were the primary outcomes for diagnostic accuracy. FPR and FLR were pre-specified composite indices and were compared with their component tests by paired DeLong tests. PRINCIPAL FINDINGS: Ferritin showed a rise-peak-fall trajectory across hospital days, peaking on Day 3 (mean 955 ± 177 ng/ml; Friedman p < 10-16); by day of illness it rose from a median of 700 ng/ml (illness days ≤3) to 950 ng/ml (days 6-7) and then plateaued. FPR Day 1 separated the severity groups with AUC 0.704 (95% CI 0.579-0.830); severe patients showed five-fold higher median FPR than non-severe patients (39.0 vs 7.5; p = 0.001). Against shock/DSS, FPR Day 1 had AUC 0.801 (95% CI 0.688-0.898), no higher than platelet count alone (AUC 0.784; DeLong p = 0.41). FLR Day 1 discriminated severe hepatitis with AUC 0.995 (95% CI 0.988-1.000), no higher than ALT alone (AUC 1.000; DeLong p = 0.23), the variable that defines this outcome. Against non-circular outcomes, Ferritin Day 1 discriminated shock/DSS with AUC 0.789 and severe thrombocytopenia with AUC 0.727. The ferritin-platelet inverse correlation was demographic-independent (partial Spearman rho=-0.392; p < 0.001). Ferritin offered no incremental benefit over platelet count alone (NRI = 0.143; IDI = 0.000). CONCLUSIONS/SIGNIFICANCE: Serum ferritin rose from admission to hospital Day 3 and was higher in patients with shock and severe thrombocytopenia. The Ferritin-to-Platelet Ratio and the Ferritin/ALT Ratio are low-cost, but neither discriminated better than the routine test it contains (platelet count or ALT). Because platelet count and transaminases entered the ratios as the lowest and highest values of the admission, and outcomes were recorded over the whole stay, both ratios are markers of disease severity rather than predictors of later shock or severe hepatitis. Prospective multi-centre studies with laboratory values and outcomes timed from admission are needed to test any predictive role.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue causes major morbidity and mortality across India, where early identification of patients progressing to dengue shock syndrome or severe hepatitis remains an unmet need. We characterised serial ferritin kinetics and evaluated two ferritin-derived ratios: the Ferritin-to-Platelet Ratio (FPR) and the Ferritin/ALT Ratio (FLR).
Design and population
This prospective observational study enrolled 100 adult WHO 2009-confirmed dengue patients at SMIMER, Surat (June 2020-November 2021). Severity was classified as non-severe (n = 68) or severe (n = 32).
Core methods
Serum ferritin was measured by sandwich ELISA on hospital Days 1, 3 and 5 and was also analysed by day of illness; platelet count and transaminases were the lowest and highest values recorded during the stay, and outcomes were recorded if present at any time between admission and discharge. The Ferritin-to-Platelet Ratio and the Ferritin/ALT Ratio are low-cost, but neither discriminated better than the routine test it contains (platelet count or ALT).
Main results
FPR Day 1 separated the severity groups with AUC 0.704 (95% CI 0.579-0.830); severe patients showed five-fold higher median FPR than non-severe patients (39.0 vs 7.5; p = 0.001). Against shock/DSS, FPR Day 1 had AUC 0.789 and severe thrombocytopenia with AUC 0.727. FLR Day 1 discriminated severe hepatitis with AUC 0.995 (95% CI 0.988-1.000), no higher than ALT alone (AUC 1.000; DeLong p = 0.23).
Interpretation and novelty
Serum ferritin rose from admission to hospital Day 3 and was higher in patients with shock and severe thrombocytopenia. The ferritin-platelet inverse correlation was demographic-independent (partial Spearman rho=-0.392; p < 0.001).
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that serum ferritin may be a useful biomarker for monitoring disease severity in hospitalised adults with dengue. However, the ratios did not offer significant improvement over existing routine tests.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its observational design and the lack of predictive value for the ratios. The evidence is moderate, as the study provides descriptive findings but does not establish causality or predictive utility.

实现更可靠的登革热早期预警:弥合理论与实践之间的差距。

Toward More Reliable Dengue Early Warning: Bridging the Gap Between Theory and Practice.
作者： Sivagnaname Narayanasamy, Yuvarajan Sivagnaname, Durga Krishnamurthy
摘要中文翻译登革热继续在全球蔓延,给热带和亚热带地区的卫生系统带来越来越大的压力。尽管在 监测、数据可用性和预测模型方面取得了进步,但早期预警往往与及时的公共卫生行动联系不够。因此,挑战不仅仅是预测,而是将异构的 监测 信息转化为可操作的情报。关键决定因素,包括气候、人口流动性、成年雌性蚊子丰度、媒介 感染、无症状感染和群体免疫力,在可行性、时空分辨率和预警价值方面各不相同。气候指标提供了重要的背景信息,但可能缺乏当地的特异性,并且有症状的病例 监测 受到确定不足和报告延迟的限制。人类流动性可以改善空间风险评估,并在选定的环境中进行预测,而成年蚊子 监测 则提供有关当地 媒介 丰度的补充信息。本研究的作者提出了一种操作优先级预警(OPEW)框架,该框架集成了哨兵 监测、互补信号的尺度感知解释、基于可行性的优先级、事件触发的强化以及 监测 阈值和预定义操作之间的显式链接。OPEW 框架不是一个经过验证的预测模型,而是一个可测试的操作框架,它根据流行病学相关性、及时性、可行性和决策价值对信息进行优先级排序。前瞻性评估应根据现有方法评估预测性能、可操作的交付时间、资源需求和公共卫生结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人类流动性可以改善空间风险评估,并在选定的环境中进行预测,而成年蚊子 监测 则提供有关当地 媒介 丰度的补充信息。
设计与对象
前瞻性评估应根据现有方法评估预测性能、可操作的交付时间、资源需求和公共卫生结果。
核心方法
OPEW 框架不是一个经过验证的预测模型,而是一个可测试的操作框架,它根据流行病学相关性、及时性、可行性和决策价值对信息进行优先级排序。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。
解释与创新
所提供的证据没有清楚地区分作者的解释或研究的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
本研究的作者提出了一种操作优先级预警(OPEW)框架,该框架集成了哨兵 监测、互补信号的尺度感知解释、基于可行性的优先级、事件触发的强化以及 监测 阈值和预定义操作之间的显式链接。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取基于检索到的全文证据。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Toward More Reliable Dengue Early Warning: Bridging the Gap Between Theory and Practice.

Authors: Sivagnaname Narayanasamy, Yuvarajan Sivagnaname, Durga Krishnamurthy
Original abstract (English)
Dengue continues to expand globally, increasing pressure on health systems across tropical and subtropical regions. Despite advances in surveillance, data availability, and predictive modeling, early warning often remains insufficiently linked to timely public health action. The challenge, therefore, is not prediction alone but translating heterogeneous surveillance information into actionable intelligence. Key determinants, including climate, human mobility, adult female mosquito abundance, vector infection, asymptomatic infection, and population immunity, vary in feasibility, spatial and temporal resolution, and early-warning value. Climate indicators provide important context but may lack local specificity, and symptomatic case surveillance is constrained by underascertainment and reporting delays. Human mobility can improve spatial risk assessment and, in selected settings, forecasting, whereas adult mosquito surveillance provides complementary information on local vector abundance. The authors of the present study propose an Operationally Prioritized Early Warning (OPEW) framework that integrates sentinel surveillance, scale-aware interpretation of complementary signals, feasibility-based prioritization, event-triggered intensification, and explicit links between surveillance thresholds and predefined actions. The OPEW framework is not a validated prediction model but a testable operational framework that prioritizes information according to epidemiological relevance, timeliness, feasibility, and decision value. Prospective evaluation should assess predictive performance, actionable lead time, resource requirements, and public health outcomes against existing approaches.

Design and population
Prospective evaluation should assess predictive performance, actionable lead time, resource requirements, and public health outcomes against existing approaches.
Core methods
The OPEW framework is not a validated prediction model but a testable operational framework that prioritizes information according to epidemiological relevance, timeliness, feasibility, and decision value.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.
Interpretation and novelty
The supplied evidence does not clearly distinguish the authors' interpretation or the study's novelty.
Scientific and public-health significance
The authors of the present study propose an Operationally Prioritized Early Warning (OPEW) framework that integrates sentinel surveillance, scale-aware interpretation of complementary signals, feasibility-based prioritization, event-triggered intensification, and explicit links between surveillance thresholds and predefined actions.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on retrieved fulltext evidence. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

哥伦比亚考卡山谷登革热时空聚集。

Spatio-temporal clustering of dengue in Valle del Cauca, Colombia.
作者： Hendrik Sy, Deninson A. Vargas-Gonzalez, Freddy Córdoba-Rentaría, Albert I. Ko, Johanna P. Daily, Neal D. Alexander
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热在哥伦比亚考卡山谷地区流行。在考卡山谷 42 个城市的市以下一级采取有效反应干预措施所需的季节性登革热 传播 的精确时间和空间规模仍不清楚。
设计与对象
使用医疗保健提供者于 2022 年向国家卫生健康系统 (SIVIGILA) 报告的个别实验室确诊的登革热病例(包括居住地理位置和就诊日期)进行了一项回顾性观察研究。其中,1,973 例发生在本研究时空分析所包含的 9 个城市的指定区域。
核心方法
使用库尔多夫的时空扫描统计分析时空聚类,以识别统计上显着的登革热聚类。使用 R 绘制结果。
主要结果
2022 年,考卡山谷报告了 3,964 例登革热病例,其中 2,232 例(56%)经过实验室确诊。我们在市以下级别确定了28个聚集性病例,半径为0-0.98公里,持续1-6个月,包含10-114例病例。26 个集群 (92.9%) 跨越暖干季和冷湿季,集群占空间分析病例的 32.0%。
解释与创新
这项研究确定了市以下级别的登革热集群,有可能为反应性登革热控制干预措施提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为反应性登革热控制干预措施提供信息,并强调登革热对哥伦比亚考卡山谷社会经济弱势群体和老年人的不成比例的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对报告数据的依赖而受到限制,但由于它基于实验室确诊病例和统计分析,因此证据有力。

Spatio-temporal clustering of dengue in Valle del Cauca, Colombia.

Authors: Hendrik Sy, Deninson A. Vargas-Gonzalez, Freddy Córdoba-Rentaría, Albert I. Ko, Johanna P. Daily, Neal D. Alexander
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue is endemic in Colombia's Valle del Cauca region. The precise timing and spatial scale of seasonal dengue transmission necessary for effective reactive interventions at the sub-municipal level across the 42 municipalities of Valle del Cauca remain unclear. This study aimed to identify spatio-temporal dengue clusters in Valle del Cauca. METHODOLOGY/PRINCIPAL FINDINGS: A retrospective observational study was conducted using individual laboratory-confirmed dengue cases, including residential geolocation and consultation dates, reported by healthcare providers to the Sistema Nacional de Vigilancia en Salud Pública (SIVIGILA) in 2022. Residence was utilized as the probable site of infection. Spatio-temporal clustering was analyzed using Kulldorff's space-time scan statistic to identify statistically significant dengue clusters. Results were mapped using R. As a second aim, the association between clinical and demographic characteristics and dengue hospitalization was evaluated. Of 3,964 dengue cases reported in Valle del Cauca in 2022, 2,232 (56%) were laboratory-confirmed. Of these, 1,973 occurred in the defined areas of the nine municipalities included in the spatio-temporal analysis of this study. We identified 28 clusters at the sub-municipal level, with radii of 0-0.98 km, lasting 1-6 months and containing 10-114 cases. Twenty-six clusters (92.9%) spanned both warm-dry and cool-wet seasons, and clusters accounted for 32.0% of spatially analyzed cases. 960 patients were hospitalized. Lower socioeconomic status (OR 1.30, 95% CI 1.07-1.57), enrollment in the state-subsidized insurance scheme (OR 1.74, 95% CI 1.41-2.15) compared with the contributory scheme, and age ≥ 70 years compared to ages 20-29 years (OR 2.47, 95% CI 1.51-4.04) were associated with higher odds of hospitalization. CONCLUSIONS/SIGNIFICANCE: This study identified dengue clusters at the sub-municipal level with the potential to inform reactive dengue control interventions and demonstrated a disproportionate impact on socioeconomically disadvantaged populations and the elderly in Valle del Cauca, Colombia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is endemic in Colombia's Valle del Cauca region. The precise timing and spatial scale of seasonal dengue transmission necessary for effective reactive interventions at the sub-municipal level across the 42 municipalities of Valle del Cauca remain unclear.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study was conducted using individual laboratory-confirmed dengue cases, including residential geolocation and consultation dates, reported by healthcare providers to the Sistema Nacional de Vigilancia en Salud Pública (SIVIGILA) in 2022. Of these, 1,973 occurred in the defined areas of the nine municipalities included in the spatio-temporal analysis of this study.
Core methods
Spatio-temporal clustering was analyzed using Kulldorff's space-time scan statistic to identify statistically significant dengue clusters. Results were mapped using R.
Main results
Of 3,964 dengue cases reported in Valle del Cauca in 2022, 2,232 (56%) were laboratory-confirmed. We identified 28 clusters at the sub-municipal level, with radii of 0-0.98 km, lasting 1-6 months and containing 10-114 cases. Twenty-six clusters (92.9%) spanned both warm-dry and cool-wet seasons, and clusters accounted for 32.0% of spatially analyzed cases.
Interpretation and novelty
This study identified dengue clusters at the sub-municipal level with the potential to inform reactive dengue control interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform reactive dengue control interventions and highlight the disproportionate impact on socioeconomically disadvantaged populations and the elderly in Valle del Cauca, Colombia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on reported data, but the evidence is strong as it is based on laboratory-confirmed cases and statistical analysis.

气候变化对巴基斯坦信德省 登革病毒 案例 发病率 的影响:2022 年后洪水。

The impact of climate variability on dengue virus case incidence in Sindh, Pakistan: Post-2022 flooding.
作者： Amna Tariq, Raheel Allana, Sajid Shah, Momin Kazi, Desiree LaBeaud
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解气候变化,特别是洪水、降雨和温度波动,如何影响巴基斯坦信德省的 登革病毒 案例 发病率。登革病毒 是一种自限性病毒性疾病,主要存在于热带和亚热带地区,对公共卫生产生重大影响。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2020 年至 2025 年间巴基斯坦信德省每月的 登革病毒 疾病数据。研究重点是气候因素对该地区登革热病例 发病率 的影响,特别是季风后月份。
核心方法
研究人员采用参数广义线性模型和广义加性模型来评估气候变量与登革热发病率之间的关系。他们研究了降雨量和温度对病例数的影响,其中降雨量存在 1 个月的滞后性。
主要结果
Monthly confirmed dengue cases showed strong seasonality, with the majority occurring in post-monsoon months (September-November). A 1-month lag in rainfall was associated with a statistically significant increase in dengue incidence (IRR = 1.008, 95% CI: 1.003-1.012, p = 0.002), corresponding to a 0.8% increase in expected case counts per unit (mm) increase in rainfall one month prior.
解释与创新
研究结果表明,降雨,尤其是滞后 1 个月的降雨,对登革热 发病率 有显着影响,凸显了气候因素在疾病动态中的重要性。这项研究为信德省气候变化与登革热 暴发 之间的关系提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为登革热 监测 的公共卫生策略和气候变化地区的预防提供参考,特别是在巴基斯坦信德省。了解这些关系可能有助于预测和缓解未来的 暴发。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于每月病例数据,并未考虑影响登革热 发病率 的其他潜在因素。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于未经实验验证的观察数据和统计模型。

The impact of climate variability on dengue virus case incidence in Sindh, Pakistan: Post-2022 flooding.

Authors: Amna Tariq, Raheel Allana, Sajid Shah, Momin Kazi, Desiree LaBeaud
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus is spread primarily by the Aedes aegypti and Ae. albopictus mosquitoes is a self-limiting viral disease primarily present in the tropical and subtropical regions of the world. Temperature and rainfall impact vector development, virus replication, and habitat suitability for Ae. albopictus larvae and pupae. Pakistan, located in Southeast Asia, is one country where DENV is endemic and impacts thousands of lives each year. In recent years, Pakistan has been hardly hit by climate change, with heavy rainfalls and flooding recurring each year, with simultaneous surges of dengue virus across the country. In this paper, we analyze the monthly dengue virus disease data from Sindh Pakistan, from 2020-2025 to understand the impacts of flooding, rainfall, and temperature fluctuations on dengue case incidence in the region. We use parametric generalized linear models and the generalized additive model. Our results indicate that monthly confirmed dengue cases exhibit a strong seasonality, with the great majority of cases concentrated in the post-monsoon months (September-November). A 1-month lag in rainfall was associated with a statistically significant increase in dengue incidence (IRR = 1.008, 95% CI: 1.003-1.012, p = 0.002), corresponding to a 0.8% increase in expected case counts per unit (mm) increase in rainfall one month prior. In contrast, neither lagged temperature term reached statistical significance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand how climate variability, specifically flooding, rainfall and temperature fluctuations, affects dengue virus case incidence in Sindh, Pakistan. Dengue virus is a self-limiting viral disease primarily present in tropical and subtropical regions, with significant public health impacts.
Design and population
The study analyzed monthly dengue virus disease data from Sindh, Pakistan, between 2020 and 2025. It focused on the impacts of climate factors on dengue case incidence in the region, with a particular emphasis on post-monsoon months.
Core methods
The researchers employed parametric generalized linear models and generalized additive models to assess the relationship between climate variables and dengue incidence. They examined the effects of rainfall and temperature on case counts, incorporating a 1-month lag for rainfall.
Main results
Monthly confirmed dengue cases showed strong seasonality, with the majority occurring in post-monsoon months (September-November). A 1-month lag in rainfall was associated with a statistically significant increase in dengue incidence (IRR = 1.008, 95% CI: 1.003-1.012, p = 0.002), corresponding to a 0.8% increase in expected case counts per unit (mm) increase in rainfall one month prior.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that rainfall, particularly with a 1-month lag, has a significant impact on dengue incidence, highlighting the importance of climate factors in disease dynamics. This study provides novel insights into the relationship between climate variability and dengue outbreaks in Sindh.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform public health strategies for dengue surveillance and prevention in regions experiencing climate variability, particularly in Sindh, Pakistan. Understanding these relationships may help in predicting and mitigating future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on monthly case data and does not account for other potential factors influencing dengue incidence. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on observational data and statistical modeling without experimental validation.

早期激活的 CD38highCD209highCD36low 单核细胞表型与 重症登革热 的进展相关

An early activated CD38highCD209highCD36low monocyte phenotype is associated with progression to severe dengue
作者： Madushika Dissanayake, Malithi De Silva, Janarthani Arulnathan, Farha Bary, Thinuri Hapuarachchi, Ayeshika Perera, Padukkage Harshani Chathurangika, Chathura Ranatunga, Damayanthi Idampitiya, Ananda Wijewickrama
摘要中文翻译背景:虽然单核细胞是 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染的主要靶细胞之一,但我们试图研究与疾病严重程度相关的单核细胞表型标记物,并评估它们作为预后生物标记物和治疗靶点的潜力。方法:通过流式细胞术评估 登革热 (DF=33) 和登革出血热 (DHF=21) 患者的单核细胞亚群和表型标记物的差异,并与健康对照 (HCs=20) 进行比较。通过将健康个体(n=8)的外周血单核细胞(PBMC)与两种血清型的DENV2、DENV3、NS1抗原和LPS共培养来进行进一步的体外实验。评估体外单核细胞中CD36和CD38表达的变化。结果:与 DF 和 HC 相比,DHF 患者中中间单核细胞显着扩张,经典单核细胞收缩,而非经典单核细胞内没有显着差异。进展为 DHF 的患者表现出活化的单核细胞表型,其特征在于 CD38high、CD209high、CD86high、HLA-DRhigh、CD36low、中间单核细胞亚群的扩增。CD38 和 CD86 在非经典单核细胞中表达最高,CD209 和 HLA-DR 在中间单核细胞中表达最高。在非经典单核细胞中观察到最显着的 CD36 下调。单核细胞(尤其是经典单核细胞)上CD38的表达与肝转氨酶和血清铁蛋白水平呈显着正相关。PBMC 与 DENV3 共培养导致 CD36 显着下调 (p=0.023)。结论:这些结果表明单核细胞激活、抗原呈递、病毒进入和脂质感应途径的早期改变有助于登革热免疫发病机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究与登革热感染疾病严重程度相关的单核细胞表型标记物,并评估它们作为预后生物标记物和治疗靶点的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究将 登革热 (DF=33) 和登革出血热 (DHF=21) 患者与健康对照 (HCs=20) 患者的单核细胞亚群和表型标记物进行了比较。
核心方法
使用流式细胞术评估 登革热 和登革出血热患者以及健康对照的单核细胞亚群和表型标记物。
主要结果
与 DF 和 HC 相比,在 DHF 患者中观察到中间单核细胞的显着扩张和经典单核细胞的收缩,而非经典单核细胞内没有显着差异。CD38 和 CD86 在非经典单核细胞中表达最高,CD209 和 HLA-DR 在中间单核细胞中表达最高。在非经典单核细胞中观察到最显着的 CD36 下调。
解释与创新
结果表明,单核细胞激活、抗原呈递、病毒进入和脂质传感途径的早期改变有助于登革热免疫发病机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为预测疾病严重程度的生物标志物的开发提供信息,并指导登革热感染的针对性治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,并且结果基于相对较小的样本量,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据的强度中等。

An early activated CD38highCD209highCD36low monocyte phenotype is associated with progression to severe dengue

Authors: Madushika Dissanayake, Malithi De Silva, Janarthani Arulnathan, Farha Bary, Thinuri Hapuarachchi, Ayeshika Perera, Padukkage Harshani Chathurangika, Chathura Ranatunga, Damayanthi Idampitiya, Ananda Wijewickrama
Original abstract (English)
Background: Although monocytes are among the principal target cells of dengue virus (DENV) infection we sought to investigate monocyte phenotypic markers associated with disease severity and evaluate their potential as prognostic biomarkers and therapeutic targets. Methods: Differences in monocyte subpopulations and phenotypic markers were assessed by flow cytometry in patients with dengue fever (DF=33) and dengue haemorrhagic fever (DHF=21) and compared to health controls (HCs=20). Further in vitro experiments were done by co-culturing peripheral blood mononucleocytes (PBMCs) of healthy individuals (n=8) with DENV2, DENV3, NS1 antigen of both serotypes and LPS. The changes in expression of CD36 and CD38 in vitro in monocytes were assessed. Results: A significant expansion of intermediate monocytes and a contraction of the classical monocytes was seen in patients with DHF compared to DF and HCs, with no significant differences within the non-classical monocytes. Patients who progressed to develop DHF, exhibited an activated monocyte phenotype characterized by, expansion of CD38high, CD209high, CD86high, HLA-DRhigh, CD36low, intermediate monocyte subset. The highest expression of CD38 and CD86 was seen in non-classical monocytes, and CD209 and HLA-DR in intermediate monocytes. Most significant downregulation of CD36 was observed in non-classical monocytes. CD38 expression on monocytes (especially classical monocytes) positively and significantly correlated with liver transaminases and serum ferritin levels. Co-culture of PBMCs with DENV3 led to significant downregulation of CD36 (p=0.023). Conclusions: These results suggest that early alterations in monocyte activation, antigen presentation, viral entry, and lipid sensing pathways contribute to dengue immunopathogenesis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate monocyte phenotypic markers associated with disease severity in dengue infection and evaluate their potential as prognostic biomarkers and therapeutic targets.
Design and population
The study compared monocyte subpopulations and phenotypic markers in patients with dengue fever (DF=33) and dengue haemorrhagic fever (DHF=21) to health controls (HCs=20).
Core methods
Monocyte subpopulations and phenotypic markers were assessed using flow cytometry in patients with dengue fever and dengue haemorrhagic fever, as well as in health controls.
Main results
A significant expansion of intermediate monocytes and a contraction of classical monocytes was observed in DHF patients compared to DF and HCs, with no significant differences within the non-classical monocytes. The highest expression of CD38 and CD86 was seen in non-classical monocytes, and CD209 and HLA-DR in intermediate monocytes. Most significant downregulation of CD36 was observed in non-classical monocytes.
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest that early alterations in monocyte activation, antigen presentation, viral entry, and lipid sensing pathways contribute to dengue immunopathogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of biomarkers for predicting disease severity and guide targeted therapeutic strategies in dengue infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on a relatively small sample size, which may affect the generalisability of the findings. The evidence is moderate in strength.

CBC 衍生评分与达卡疑似登革热患者登革热 NS1 抗原阳性相关:一项回顾性模型开发研究

A CBC-derived score associated with dengue NS1 antigen positivity among suspected dengue patients in Dhaka: a retrospective model-development study
作者： Rasel Khan Nishan, Md. Shaha Alam, Abul Kalam Azad, Md. Murshed Hasan Sarkar, Tanjum Ahmed Nodee, Md. Zakaria Mia, Md. Ibrahim Miah, Md. Shariful Islam, M. A. Khan, Nishan, R. K.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在高容量环境中,对疑似登革热进行早期诊断分类非常重要,但确认性检测可能会延迟或间歇性进行。
设计与对象
本研究旨在开发并内部验证一种简单的全血细胞计数 (CBC) 衍生评分,以估计孟加拉国达卡疑似登革热患者当天登革热非结构蛋白 1 (NS1) 抗原阳性的可能性。
核心方法
最小绝对收缩和选择算子(LASSO)惩罚逻辑回归用于常规可用的人口统计和血液学预测因子中的变量选择。使用未惩罚的逻辑回归对选定的预测变量进行重新拟合,以得出 CBC 分数。使用 400 个重新采样的引导乐观校正来评估内部验证和校准。
主要结果
Among 596 patients, 306 (51.3%) were NS1 positive. The final parsimonious model retained platelet count rescaled per 10 x 10 9 /L, white blood cell count, and monocyte count. The optimism-corrected AUC was 0.9429. At the Youden-derived cutoff of 11.825, the final integer score had sensitivity 0.833 (95% CI, 0.787-0.873), specificity 0.966 (95% CI, 0.938-0.983), PPV 0.962 (95% CI, 0.932-0.982), NPV 0.846 (95% CI, 0.802-0.883), and accuracy 0.898 (95% CI, 0.870-0.921). A rounded cutoff of 12 produced similar performance. In benchmark analysis, the final model showed a modest improvement over platelet count alone (AUC 0.944 vs 0.931; Delta AUC 0.012; DeLong p=0.00364).
解释与创新
三参数 CBC 评分可以估计该单中心数据集中疑似登革热患者 NS1 抗原阳性的可能性。
科研与公卫意义
该分数应被解释为仅 CBC 的分类辅助手段,而不是替代登革热验证性检测。
局限与证据强度
在常规临床使用之前,需要在独立的 队列研究 中进行外部验证。

A CBC-derived score associated with dengue NS1 antigen positivity among suspected dengue patients in Dhaka: a retrospective model-development study

Authors: Rasel Khan Nishan, Md. Shaha Alam, Abul Kalam Azad, Md. Murshed Hasan Sarkar, Tanjum Ahmed Nodee, Md. Zakaria Mia, Md. Ibrahim Miah, Md. Shariful Islam, M. A. Khan, Nishan, R. K.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Early diagnostic triage for suspected dengue is important in high-volume settings, yet confirmatory testing may be delayed or intermittently available. This study aimed to develop and internally validate a simple complete blood count (CBC)-derived score to estimate the likelihood of same-day dengue non-structural protein 1 (NS1) antigen positivity among suspected dengue patients in Dhaka, Bangladesh. Methods We conducted a retrospective model-development study at Uttara Adhunik Medical College Hospital using data from patients with clinical suspicion of dengue between 19 August and 12 October 2023. The final analysis included 596 unique patients who underwent CBC and NS1 testing on the same day. Least absolute shrinkage and selection operator (LASSO) penalized logistic regression was used for variable selection among routinely available demographic and hematological predictors. Selected predictors were refit using unpenalized logistic regression to derive a CBC score. Internal validation and calibration were assessed using bootstrap optimism correction with 400 resamples. Results Among 596 patients, 306 (51.3%) were NS1 positive. The final parsimonious model retained platelet count rescaled per 10 x 10 9 /L, white blood cell count, and monocyte count. The optimism-corrected AUC was 0.9429. At the Youden-derived cutoff of 11.825, the final integer score had sensitivity 0.833 (95% CI, 0.787-0.873), specificity 0.966 (95% CI, 0.938-0.983), PPV 0.962 (95% CI, 0.932-0.982), NPV 0.846 (95% CI, 0.802-0.883), and accuracy 0.898 (95% CI, 0.870-0.921). A rounded cutoff of 12 produced similar performance. In benchmark analysis, the final model showed a modest improvement over platelet count alone (AUC 0.944 vs 0.931; Delta AUC 0.012; DeLong p=0.00364). Conclusion A three-parameter CBC score can estimate the likelihood of NS1 antigen positivity among suspected dengue patients in this single-center dataset. The score should be interpreted as a CBC-only triage adjunct, not as a replacement for confirmatory dengue testing. External validation in independent cohorts is needed before routine clinical use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Early diagnostic triage for suspected dengue is important in high-volume settings, yet confirmatory testing may be delayed or intermittently available.
Design and population
This study aimed to develop and internally validate a simple complete blood count (CBC)-derived score to estimate the likelihood of same-day dengue non-structural protein 1 (NS1) antigen positivity among suspected dengue patients in Dhaka, Bangladesh.
Core methods
Least absolute shrinkage and selection operator (LASSO) penalized logistic regression was used for variable selection among routinely available demographic and hematological predictors. Selected predictors were refit using unpenalized logistic regression to derive a CBC score. Internal validation and calibration were assessed using bootstrap optimism correction with 400 resamples.
Main results
Among 596 patients, 306 (51.3%) were NS1 positive. The final parsimonious model retained platelet count rescaled per 10 x 10 9 /L, white blood cell count, and monocyte count. The optimism-corrected AUC was 0.9429. At the Youden-derived cutoff of 11.825, the final integer score had sensitivity 0.833 (95% CI, 0.787-0.873), specificity 0.966 (95% CI, 0.938-0.983), PPV 0.962 (95% CI, 0.932-0.982), NPV 0.846 (95% CI, 0.802-0.883), and accuracy 0.898 (95% CI, 0.870-0.921). A rounded cutoff of 12 produced similar performance. In benchmark analysis, the final model showed a modest improvement over platelet count alone (AUC 0.944 vs 0.931; Delta AUC 0.012; DeLong p=0.00364).
Interpretation and novelty
A three-parameter CBC score can estimate the likelihood of NS1 antigen positivity among suspected dengue patients in this single-center dataset.
Scientific and public-health significance
The score should be interpreted as a CBC-only triage adjunct, not as a replacement for confirmatory dengue testing.
Limitations and evidence strength
External validation in independent cohorts is needed before routine clinical use.
