使用实时医疗保健提供者反馈来指导登革热 暴发 期间的应对工作,美属维尔京群岛,2024-2025 年。

Using Real-Time Health Care Provider Feedback to Guide Response Efforts During a Dengue Outbreak, US Virgin Islands, 2024-2025.
作者： Parker K Acevedo, Valerie Mac, Annellie Gumbs, Sarah Gallalee, Janice Perez-Padilla, Joshua M Wong, Lisa Ekpo, Cosme Harrison, Giulia Earle-Richardson, Laura E Adams
摘要中文翻译2024 年,美属维尔京群岛 (USVI) 发生了登革热 暴发。为了指导应对工作,我们在正在进行的 暴发 期间评估了医疗保健提供者与登革热诊断、临床管理和报告相关的做法、经验和需求。通过半结构化访谈,对美属维尔京群岛的 9 名临床医生进行了快速需求评估。访谈数据被编码到预定义的主题领域并进行分析,以确定关键差距和可行的支持机会。结果揭示了临床培训、诊断支持和临床医生社区参与方面的差距。提供商表示,他们对识别警告信号的信心有限,并表示需要额外的培训和决策工具。研究结果表明,临床医生经常依赖对 诊断 的临床判断,但面临与诊断测试相关的挑战,包括成本、周转时间长和测试选择的不确定性。还发现了社区对登革热 传播 的认识差距和误解。快速需求评估由维尔京群岛卫生部和疾病控制与预防中心牵头,结果为快速实施有针对性的干预措施提供了依据,包括临床医生培训资源、扩大诊断测试途径以及加强社区外展工作,例如简化诊断测试流程和社区外展。本案例研究强调了快速评估在通过以提供者为中心的反馈指导 暴发 期间及时公共卫生应对方面的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2024 年,美属维尔京群岛 (USVI) 发生了登革热 暴发。该研究旨在通过评估卫生保健提供者在 暴发 期间与登革热诊断、临床管理和报告相关的做法、经验和需求来指导应对工作。
设计与对象
在登革热 暴发 期间,我们对美属维尔京群岛的 9 名临床医生进行了半结构化访谈,进行了快速需求评估。
核心方法
访谈数据被编码到预定义的主题领域并进行分析,以确定关键差距和可行的支持机会。
主要结果
结果揭示了临床培训、诊断支持和临床医生社区参与方面的差距。提供商表示,他们对识别警告信号的信心有限,并表示需要额外的培训和决策工具。研究结果表明,临床医生经常依赖对 诊断 的临床判断,但面临与诊断测试相关的挑战,包括成本、周转时间长和测试选择的不确定性。
解释与创新
该研究强调了快速评估在通过以提供者为中心的反馈指导 暴发 期间及时公共卫生应对方面的价值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的干预措施提供信息,包括临床医生培训资源、扩大诊断测试途径以及加强社区外展工作,例如简化诊断测试流程和社区宣传活动。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和依赖临床医生自我报告数据的限制,这可能会引入偏差。由于评估的定性性质,证据的强度中等。

Using Real-Time Health Care Provider Feedback to Guide Response Efforts During a Dengue Outbreak, US Virgin Islands, 2024-2025.

Authors: Parker K Acevedo, Valerie Mac, Annellie Gumbs, Sarah Gallalee, Janice Perez-Padilla, Joshua M Wong, Lisa Ekpo, Cosme Harrison, Giulia Earle-Richardson, Laura E Adams
Original abstract (English)
During 2024, a dengue outbreak occurred in the US Virgin Islands (USVI). To guide response efforts, we assessed health care providers' practices, experiences, and needs related to dengue diagnostics, clinical management, and reporting during the ongoing outbreak. A rapid needs assessment was conducted using semi-structured interviews with 9 clinicians across the USVI. Interview data were coded into predefined topic areas and analyzed to identify key gaps and actionable opportunities for support. Outcomes revealed gaps in clinical training, diagnostic support, and clinician-community engagement. Providers reported limited confidence in recognizing warning signs and expressed a need for additional training and decision-making tools. Findings indicated that clinicians often relied on clinical judgment for diagnosis but faced challenges related to diagnostic testing, including cost, long turnaround times, and uncertainty about test selection. Gaps in community awareness and misconceptions about dengue transmission were also identified. The rapid needs assessment was led by the Virgin Islands Department of Health and the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention, and results informed rapid implementation of targeted interventions, including clinician training resources, expanded diagnostic testing pathways, and enhanced community outreach efforts, such as streamlined diagnostic testing processes and community outreach. This case study highlights the value of rapid assessments in guiding timely public health responses during outbreaks through provider-centered feedback.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
During 2024, a dengue outbreak occurred in the US Virgin Islands (USVI). The study aimed to guide response efforts by assessing health care providers' practices, experiences, and needs related to dengue diagnostics, clinical management, and reporting during the ongoing outbreak.
Design and population
A rapid needs assessment was conducted using semi-structured interviews with 9 clinicians across the US Virgin Islands during the dengue outbreak.
Core methods
Interview data were coded into predefined topic areas and analyzed to identify key gaps and actionable opportunities for support.
Main results

Providers reported limited confidence in recognizing warning signs and expressed a need for additional training and decision-making tools. Findings indicated that clinicians often relied on clinical judgment for diagnosis but faced challenges related to diagnostic testing, including cost, long turnaround times, and uncertainty about test selection.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the value of rapid assessments in guiding timely public health responses during outbreaks through provider-centered feedback.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted interventions, including clinician training resources, expanded diagnostic testing pathways, and enhanced community outreach efforts, such as streamlined diagnostic testing processes and community awareness campaigns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and reliance on self-reported data from clinicians, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the qualitative nature of the assessment.

Divergências in silico nos sítios de RT-qPCR do vírus da dengue e correção do conjunto afetado 登革病毒 的 RT-qPCR 退火位点的计算机分歧和受影响的引物探针组的校正

Divergências in silico nos sítios de anelamento de RT-qPCR do vírus da dengue e correção do conjunto afetado In silico divergences at RT-qPCR annealing sites of dengue virus and correction of the affected primer-probe set
作者： Ana Maria Nogueira Borges, Carlos Eduardo Pimenta Dias, Maria Bárbara de Holanda Cavalcante Ubaldo Monteiro e Barbosa, Paulo Henrique da Silva Marques, Matheus Martins Collicchio Daúde
摘要中文翻译登革热包含具有广泛遗传多样性的四种血清型,引物和探针识别区域中的 突变 可能会损害 RT-qPCR 检测的可靠性。这项研究评估了这种差异对 2005 年发表的用于 DENV 血清型特异性检测的四重 RT-qPCR 检测的退火位点的影响,并针对受影响最严重的血清型提出了校正集。这是一项采用计算机方法的描述性分析研究。从 NCBI 检索了 24 个完整的 基因组,每种血清型 6 个,并形成了主面板,扩展到 45 个 DENV-3 和 48 个 DENV-2 基因组 以进行详细分析。通过针对两条链的局部比对,将测定的 12 个寡核苷酸定位在每个 基因组 中,并根据原始方案中发布的位置验证坐标。在所有十二种寡核苷酸中观察到差异。7 个事件落在引物 3' 端的 5 个碱基内,分布在 DENV-2、DENV-3 和 DENV-4 中,DENV-1 中没有。有义引物 D3-F 被证明是最受影响的组件,因为来自 3' 端的两个碱基的位置 18 的取代存在于分析的 45 个 DENV-3 基因组 中的 45 个中,来自不同的国家和收集时期,因此所发表的寡核苷酸在其中没有一个显示出完美的配对。事实证明,对 DENV-3 装置进行彻底重新设计是不可行的。在 10,707 个 基因组 位置中,有 8,268 个 (77.2%) 是保守的,但只有 23 个序列达到 18 个核苷酸,并且只有 3 个 宿主 寡核苷酸能够区分其余血清型,彼此之间相隔数千个碱基。在 3' 非翻译区中,生成的所有 25 个组均包含至少一个与另一种血清型相同的成分。因此,通过简并碱基对现有寡核苷酸进行了修正,将 D3-F 的完美配对从无配对提高到 45 个 基因组 中的 44 个。结论是,血清型内保守性和血清型特异性竞争相同的基因组区域,并且诊断位点的监测应纳入常规 基因组监测 中。

问题与背景
登革热包含具有广泛遗传多样性的四种血清型,引物和探针识别区域中的 突变 可能会损害 RT-qPCR 检测的可靠性。
设计与对象
这项研究评估了这种差异对 2005 年发表的用于 DENV 血清型特异性检测的四重 RT-qPCR 检测的退火位点的影响,并针对受影响最严重的血清型提出了校正集。
核心方法
通过针对两条链的局部比对,将测定的 12 个寡核苷酸定位在每个 基因组 中,并根据原始方案中发布的位置验证坐标。
主要结果
在所有十二种寡核苷酸中观察到差异。7 个事件落在引物 3' 端的 5 个碱基内,分布在 DENV-2、DENV-3 和 DENV-4 中,DENV-1 中没有。
解释与创新
有义引物 D3-F 被证明是最受影响的组件,因为来自 3' 端的两个碱基的位置 18 的取代存在于分析的 45 个 DENV-3 基因组 中的 45 个中,来自不同的国家和收集时期,因此所发表的寡核苷酸在其中没有一个显示出完美的配对。
科研与公卫意义
血清型内保守性和血清型特异性竞争相同的基因组区域,并且诊断位点的监测应纳入常规 基因组监测 中。
局限与证据强度
事实证明,对 DENV-3 装置进行彻底重新设计是不可行的。在 10,707 个 基因组 位置中,有 8,268 个 (77.2%) 是保守的,但只有 23 个序列达到 18 个核苷酸,并且只有 3 个 宿主 寡核苷酸能够区分其余血清型,彼此之间相隔数千个碱基。

Divergências in silico nos sítios de anelamento de RT-qPCR do vírus da dengue e correção do conjunto afetado In silico divergences at RT-qPCR annealing sites of dengue virus and correction of the affected primer-probe set

Authors: Ana Maria Nogueira Borges, Carlos Eduardo Pimenta Dias, Maria Bárbara de Holanda Cavalcante Ubaldo Monteiro e Barbosa, Paulo Henrique da Silva Marques, Matheus Martins Collicchio Daúde
Original abstract (English)
Dengue comprises four serotypes with wide genetic diversity, and mutations in the regions recognised by primers and probes may compromise the reliability of RT-qPCR assays. This study assessed the impact of such divergences on the annealing sites of a fourplex RT-qPCR assay for serotype-specific detection of DENV, published in 2005, and proposed a corrected set for the most affected serotype. This is a descriptive-analytical study with an in silico approach. Twenty-four complete genomes, six per serotype, were retrieved from NCBI and formed the main panel, expanded to 45 DENV-3 and 48 DENV-2 genomes for the detailed analyses. The twelve oligonucleotides of the assay were located in each genome by local alignment against both strands, with coordinates validated against the positions published in the original protocol. Divergences were observed in all twelve oligonucleotides. Seven events fell within the five bases of the 3′ end of primers, distributed across DENV-2, DENV-3 and DENV-4, with none in DENV-1. The sense primer D3-F proved the most compromised component, since the substitution at position 18, two bases from the 3′ end, was present in 45 of the 45 DENV-3 genomes analysed, from different countries and collection periods, so that the published oligonucleotide shows perfect pairing in none of them. Complete redesign of the DENV-3 set proved unfeasible. Of the 10,707 genome positions, 8,268 (77.2%) are conserved, yet only 23 stretches reach 18 nucleotides and merely three can host an oligonucleotide able to discriminate the remaining serotypes, separated from one another by thousands of bases. In the 3′ untranslated region, all 25 sets generated contained at least one component identical to another serotype. Correction of the existing oligonucleotides through degenerate bases was therefore adopted, raising perfect pairing of D3-F from none to 44 of the 45 genomes. It is concluded that intra-serotypic conservation and serotype specificity compete for the same genomic region and that monitoring of diagnostic sites should be incorporated into routine genomic surveillance.

揭示 NIRAGA 茶复方作为抗氧化剂和 抗病毒药物 抗登革热药物的潜力:来自体外和计算机方法的见解

UNCOVERING THE POTENTIAL OF NIRAGA TEA POLYHERBALTM AS AN ANTIOXIDANT AND ANTIVIRAL AGENT AGAINST DENGUE: INSIGHTS FROM IN VITRO AND IN SILICO APPROACHES
作者： Muhammad Faris Rizqullah, Nurul Fathiah Azzahra, Kireina Nurfadhillah, Arum Kinasih Ramadhani, Anjeli Bismi Arsy, I Made Dwi Mertha Adnyana, Radinal Kautsar
摘要中文翻译登革热是一种 媒介 传播的疾病,在热带地区造成很高的健康负担,但目前尚无特定的 抗病毒药物 治疗方法。本研究旨在研究 Niraga Tea PolyherbalTM 配方的抗氧化活性、细胞毒性和针对 登革病毒 血清型 2 (DENV-2) 的 抗病毒药物 活性,并进行植物化学分析和分子对接。使用GC-MS和FT-IR分析来分析生物活性化合物的概况和结构;使用DPPH、ABTS和FRAP方法评价抗氧化活性;使用 Viral ToxGlo 测定评估登革热 抗病毒药物 活性;并使用 Vero 细胞在 MTT 测定中评估细胞毒性。在计算机上对靶向 DENV-2 NS5 蛋白的化合物进行分子对接评估。GC-MS 表征显示了 100 种化合物,主要是油酸甲酯 (69.60%) 和十五烷酸乙酯 (20.84%),以及槲皮素 (0.46%) 和表没食子儿茶素没食子酸酯 (0.19%)。FT-IR 分析证实酚羟基是主要官能团。DPPH、ABTS 和 FRAP 测定显示 IC50 值分别为 20.79、21.34 和 18.80 μg/mL。Vero 细胞的 MTT 测定得出的 CC50 值为 1207 μg/mL。Niraga Tea PolyherbalTM 显着抑制细胞病变效应(F= 42.50;p=0.005),针对 DENV-2 的 EC50 为 10.32 μg/mL,选择性指数 (SI) 为 116.96。油酸甲酯和十五烷酸乙酯与 DENV-2 NS5 的分子结合研究揭示了较弱的结合亲和力(ΔG -3.95 至 -4.31 kcal/mol),比用更有效的体外化合物观察到的结合亲和力弱。总之,Niraga Tea PolyherbalTM 有潜力被开发为登革热 抗病毒药物 治疗的候选植物药物,并且对 宿主 细胞具有高安全性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是一种 媒介 传播的疾病,在热带地区造成很高的健康负担,但目前尚无特定的 抗病毒药物 治疗方法。

本研究旨在研究 Niraga Tea PolyherbalTM 配方的抗氧化活性、细胞毒性和针对 登革病毒 血清型 2 (DENV-2) 的 抗病毒药物 活性,并进行植物化学分析和分子对接。
核心方法

主要结果
GC-MS 表征显示了 100 种化合物,主要是油酸甲酯 (69.60%) 和十五烷酸乙酯 (20.84%),以及槲皮素 (0.46%) 和表没食子儿茶素没食子酸酯 (0.19%)。

Niraga Tea PolyherbalTM 显着抑制细胞病变效应(F= 42.50;p=0.005),针对 DENV-2 的 EC50 为 10.32 μg/mL,选择性指数 (SI) 为 116.96。

油酸甲酯和十五烷酸乙酯与 DENV-2 NS5 的分子结合研究揭示了较弱的结合亲和力(ΔG -3.95 至 -4.31 kcal/mol),比用更有效的体外化合物观察到的结合亲和力弱。

UNCOVERING THE POTENTIAL OF NIRAGA TEA POLYHERBALTM AS AN ANTIOXIDANT AND ANTIVIRAL AGENT AGAINST DENGUE: INSIGHTS FROM IN VITRO AND IN SILICO APPROACHES

Authors: Muhammad Faris Rizqullah, Nurul Fathiah Azzahra, Kireina Nurfadhillah, Arum Kinasih Ramadhani, Anjeli Bismi Arsy, I Made Dwi Mertha Adnyana, Radinal Kautsar
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is a vector-borne disease with a high health burden in tropical regions, yet no specific antiviral therapy is currently available. This study aimed to investigate the antioxidant activity, cytotoxicity, and antiviral activity against dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV-2) of the Niraga Tea PolyherbalTM formulation and performed phytochemical profiling and molecular docking. GC‒MS and FT‒IR analyses were used to analyze the profiles and structures of bioactive compounds; antioxidant activity was evaluated using the DPPH, ABTS, and FRAP methods; dengue antiviral activity was evaluated using the Viral ToxGlo assay; and cytotoxicity was evaluated using Vero cells in an MTT assay. Molecular docking evaluations of the compounds targeting the DENV-2 NS5 protein in silico. GC‒MS characterization revealed 100 compounds, predominantly methyl oleate (69.60%) and ethyl pentadecanoate (20.84%), along with quercetin (0.46%) and epigallocatechin gallate (0.19%). FT-IR analysis confirmed that phenolic hydroxyl groups were the dominant functional groups. DPPH, ABTS, and FRAP assays revealed IC50 values of 20.79, 21.34, and 18.80 μg/mL, respectively. MTT assays of Vero cells yielded a CC50 value of 1207 μg/mL. Niraga Tea PolyherbalTM significantly inhibited the cytopathic effects (F= 42.50; p=0.005), with an EC50 of 10.32 μg/mL and a selectivity index (SI) of 116.96 against DENV-2. Molecular binding studies of methyl oleate and ethyl pentadecanoate to DENV-2 NS5 revealed weak binding affinities (ΔG -3.95 to -4.31 kcal/mol), which were weaker than those observed with more potent in vitro compounds. In conclusion, Niraga Tea PolyherbalTM has the potential to be developed as a candidate phytopharmaceutical for dengue antiviral therapy with a high safety profile against host cells.

C6/36 和 Vero 细胞中的 登革病毒 复制需要前膜蛋白的糖基化

Glycosylation of the Premembrane Protein Is Required for Dengue Virus Replication in Both C6/36 and Vero Cells
作者： Z. Beau Reneer, Stephanie Dong, Long-Ping V. Tse, Ralph Steven Baric
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨 登革病毒 (DENV) prM 蛋白上的 N69 糖基化位点对病毒复制和感染性的重要性。登革病毒 是一种蚊媒、正义、单链 RNA 病毒,包括四种血清型 (DENV1-4),在血清型内进一步分为不同的基因型。
设计与对象
该研究涉及通过在 N69 糖基化位点引入单个氨基酸 突变 来生成 DENV2 和 DENV4 的功能丧失病毒。测试了这些突变病毒在蚊子细胞中的体外复制能力,并与野生型登革热病毒株进行了比较。
核心方法
研究人员通过在 69 位点引入单个氨基酸 突变 来创建 N69 糖基化敲除病毒。然后,他们评估了蚊子细胞中的病毒复制,并评估了这些突变病毒与野生型 DENV 毒株相比的感染性。
主要结果
尽管在体外多次传代,两种 N69 糖基化敲除病毒都没有可检测到的滴度。其中一种近端糖基化突变体的病毒复制水平接近野生型 (WT) DENV 毒株。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,在 prM 蛋白 69 位保留 N 连接聚糖对于蚊子细胞的 DENV 感染性至关重要。他们还指出,在 69 位附近插入糖基化位点可能会恢复某些 DENV4 菌株的感染性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 DENV 复制和感染性的分子机制的见解,这可以为靶向 抗病毒药物 策略的开发提供信息,并提高对病毒 进化 和 发病机制 的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外实验,可能无法完全代表体内条件。证据是中等的,因为它基于实验室观察,没有临床或流行病学数据。

Glycosylation of the Premembrane Protein Is Required for Dengue Virus Replication in Both C6/36 and Vero Cells

Authors: Z. Beau Reneer, Stephanie Dong, Long-Ping V. Tse, Ralph Steven Baric
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) is a mosquito-borne, positive sense, single-stranded RNA virus that includes four serotypes (DENV1-4) that are further divided into distinct genotypes within a serotype. DENV causes approximately 100 million human infections each year. DENV virions encode a capisd surrounded by a lipid bilayer that encodes two glycoproteins, E and prM. During virion maturation, the premembrane protein (prM) is cleaved to form a mature virion, which includes partially mature and fully mature particles. The maturity state of the DENV virion affects both the infectivity of DENVs and the ability of antibodies to neutralize the virus. The prM glycoprotein is glycosylated and highly conserved across all DENVs. In particular, the N-linked glycosylation site at position 69 is present in over 99% of DENV genomes. In this manuscript, we investigated the importance of the N69 glycosylation site by generating both DENV2 and DENV4 loss-of-function viruses. These N69 glycosylation knockout viruses were generated by introducing a single amino acid mutation at site 69. Neither mutant virus had detectable titers despite multiple passages in vitro. To further investigate the importance of the glycosylation site, we generated another variation in which the N-linked glycosylation site was repositioned to a site proximal to residue 69. One of these proximal-glycosylation mutants showed viral replication at levels that approached wild-type (WT) DENV strains. Overall, this study demonstrated the importance of retaining an N-linked glycan on the prM protein at position 69 for DENV infectivity of mosquito cells and that inserting a glycosylation site in close proximity to position 69 may restore infectivity in some DENV4 strains.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the importance of the N69 glycosylation site on the prM protein of dengue virus (DENV) for viral replication and infectivity. Dengue virus is a mosquito-borne, positive-sense, single-stranded RNA virus that includes four serotypes (DENV1-4), which are further divided into distinct genotypes within a serotype.
Design and population
The study involved generating loss-of-function viruses for DENV2 and DENV4 by introducing a single amino acid mutation at the N69 glycosylation site. These mutant viruses were tested for their ability to replicate in vitro in mosquito cells and compared to wild-type DENV strains.
Core methods
The researchers created N69 glycosylation knockout viruses by introducing a single amino acid mutation at site 69. They then assessed viral replication in mosquito cells and evaluated the infectivity of these mutant viruses compared to wild-type DENV strains.
Main results
Neither of the N69 glycosylation knockout viruses had detectable titers despite multiple passages in vitro. One of the proximal-glycosylation mutants showed viral replication at levels that approached wild-type (WT) DENV strains.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that retaining an N-linked glycan on the prM protein at position 69 is essential for DENV infectivity of mosquito cells. They also noted that inserting a glycosylation site near position 69 may restore infectivity in some DENV4 strains.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of DENV replication and infectivity, which could inform the development of targeted antiviral strategies and improve understanding of viral evolution and pathogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to in vitro experiments, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence is moderate as it is based on laboratory observations without clinical or epidemiological data.

印度尼西亚古邦市 Oesapa 卫生中心流域登革出血热病例和初级医疗保健可及性的空间测绘

Spatial Mapping of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever Cases and Primary Healthcare Accessibility in the Oesapa Health Center Catchment, Kupang City, Indonesia
作者： Yanuarius Elvis Kurniawan, Mikael Samin, Martina Ayu Sejati
摘要中文翻译登革出血热仍然是热带城市反复出现的公共卫生问题,当地的 传播 和初级医疗服务的不平等可能会延迟早期病例管理。本研究旨在绘制登革热病例的分布图,并评估印度尼西亚古邦市 Oesapa 卫生中心流域现有社区卫生服务的空间可达性。描述性定量设计与地理信息系统分析相结合。2023 年二级登革热 监测 记录、现场验证的病例坐标、行政边界和卫生设施位置均已集成到 ArcGIS 中。按性别、年龄组和 kelurahan 总结病例分布,同时使用 3,000 米的服务缓冲区来评估从 Oesapa 卫生中心到记录病例位置的潜在覆盖范围。2023 年,古邦市报告了 199 例登革热病例,其中 Oesapa 卫生中心辖区的卫生中心总数最高,有 35 例病例,占全市负担的 17.6%。在该流域内,病例集中在奥萨帕和拉西亚纳,这两个地区合计有25例病例,占当地总数的71.4%。18-59岁成年人是最多的年龄组,有21例,占60.0%,其中女性患者占57.1%。缓冲区分析表明,35 个案例中的 29 个(即 82.9%)位于 3,000 米的潜在服务范围内,其余 6 个案例位于主要缓冲区之外。研究结果表明,微尺度登革热测绘可以识别优先社区,并揭示明显服务良好的城市卫生中心区域内的剩余通道差距。

Spatial Mapping of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever Cases and Primary Healthcare Accessibility in the Oesapa Health Center Catchment, Kupang City, Indonesia

Authors: Yanuarius Elvis Kurniawan, Mikael Samin, Martina Ayu Sejati
Original abstract (English)
Dengue hemorrhagic fever remains a recurrent public health problem in tropical cities, where localized transmission and unequal access to primary healthcare can delay early case management. This study aimed to map the distribution of dengue cases and evaluate the spatial accessibility of existing community health services in the Oesapa Health Center catchment, Kupang City, Indonesia. A descriptive quantitative design was combined with geographic information system analysis. Secondary dengue surveillance records for 2023, field-verified case coordinates, administrative boundaries, and health-facility locations were integrated in ArcGIS. Case distribution was summarized by sex, age group, and kelurahan, while a 3,000-m service buffer was used to assess potential reach from Oesapa Health Center to recorded case locations. Kupang City reported 199 dengue cases in 2023, and the Oesapa Health Center catchment accounted for the highest health-center total, with 35 cases or 17.6% of the citywide burden. Within the catchment, cases were concentrated in Oesapa and Lasiana, which together contributed 25 cases or 71.4% of the local total. Adults aged 18-59 years represented the largest age group with 21 cases or 60.0%, while female patients accounted for 57.1% of cases. Buffer analysis indicated that 29 of 35 cases, or 82.9%, were located within the 3,000-m potential service catchment, leaving six cases outside the primary buffer. The findings show that micro-scale dengue mapping can identify priority neighborhoods and reveal residual access gaps within an apparently well-served urban health-center area.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue hemorrhagic fever remains a recurrent public health problem in tropical cities, where localized transmission and unequal access to primary healthcare can delay early case management.
Design and population
This study aimed to map the distribution of dengue cases and evaluate the spatial accessibility of existing community health services in the Oesapa Health Center catchment, Kupang City, Indonesia.
Core methods
A descriptive quantitative design was combined with geographic information system analysis.
Main results
Kupang City reported 199 dengue cases in 2023, and the Oesapa Health Center catchment accounted for the highest health-center total, with 35 cases or 17.6% of the citywide burden.
Interpretation and novelty
Case distribution was summarized by sex, age group, and kelurahan, while a 3,000-m service buffer was used to assess potential reach from Oesapa Health Center to recorded case locations.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

DENV-ZIKV 交叉反应对东南亚的影响:泰国的模型研究

Impact of DENV-ZIKV cross-reactivity in Southeast Asia: A modelling study in Thailand
作者： Yile Shi, Abhishek Senapati, Angkana T. Huang, Hannah E. Clapham, Huang, A. T., Clapham, H. E.
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 是东南亚 (SEA) 的流行病,那里很大一部分成年人已被感染。寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 已在该地区传播了数十年,但 传播 的范围仍不清楚。先前感染一种病毒可以预防或增强另一种病毒的感染,但人群层面的后果尚不清楚。我们开发了一个年龄结构的 DENV-ZIKV 模型,该模型具有九个交叉反应性假设,结合了交叉保护和后续增强,并模拟了泰国人口结构变化下的持续共循环。通过交叉反应,DENV 强烈塑造了 ZIKV 传播,而 ZIKV 对 DENV 传播 的影响很小。在交叉保护下,ZIKV 传播 作为罕见的 暴发 出现,长期 ZIKV 负担没有变化。当先前的 DENV 感染增加了 ZIKV 感染和症状风险时,这种负担大约增加了一倍。人口老龄化使 DENV 和 ZIKV 病例的平均年龄增加了 20 多岁,比交叉反应的影响还要大。在 ZIKV 感染和症状增强的情况下,模拟的 ZIKV 病例年龄更接近泰国 2019-2023 年报告的病例年龄。在所有假设下,超过一半的 15-49 岁人群仍然未感染 ZIKV,这使得许多怀孕者面临先天性寨卡综合征的风险。解释东南亚地区的 ZIKV 病例数需要综合考虑交叉反应、人口变化和病例检测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 是东南亚 (SEA) 的流行病,那里很大一部分成年人已被感染。先前感染一种病毒可以预防或增强另一种病毒的感染,但人群层面的后果尚不清楚。
设计与对象
我们开发了一个年龄结构的 DENV-ZIKV 模型,该模型具有九个交叉反应性假设,结合了交叉保护和后续增强,并模拟了泰国人口结构变化下的持续共循环。
核心方法
人口老龄化使 DENV 和 ZIKV 病例的平均年龄增加了 20 多岁,比交叉反应的影响还要大。在 ZIKV 感染和症状增强的情况下,模拟的 ZIKV 病例年龄更接近泰国 2019-2023 年报告的病例年龄。
主要结果
通过交叉反应,DENV 强烈塑造了 ZIKV 传播,而 ZIKV 对 DENV 传播 的影响很小。在交叉保护下,ZIKV 传播 作为罕见的 暴发 出现,长期 ZIKV 负担没有变化。当先前的 DENV 感染增加了 ZIKV 感染和症状风险时,这种负担大约增加了一倍。
解释与创新
解释东南亚地区的 ZIKV 病例数需要综合考虑交叉反应、人口变化和病例检测。在所有假设下,超过一半的 15-49 岁人群仍然未感染 ZIKV,这使得许多怀孕者面临先天性寨卡综合征的风险。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了在解释东南亚寨卡病毒病例数时考虑交叉反应和人口变化的重要性。他们还强调了母亲未接触过寨卡病毒的妊娠期先天性寨卡综合症的公共健康风险。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。根据模型的假设和模拟结果,置信度很高。

Impact of DENV-ZIKV cross-reactivity in Southeast Asia: A modelling study in Thailand

Authors: Yile Shi, Abhishek Senapati, Angkana T. Huang, Hannah E. Clapham, Huang, A. T., Clapham, H. E.
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) is endemic across Southeast Asia (SEA), where a large proportion of adults have been infected. Zika virus (ZIKV) has circulated in the region for decades but the extent of transmission remains unclear. Prior infection with one virus can protect against or enhance infection with the other, but the population-level consequences are unknown. We developed an age-structured DENV-ZIKV model with nine cross-reactivity assumptions combining cross-protection and subsequent enhancement, and simulated sustained co-circulation under changing Thai demography. Through cross-reactivity, DENV strongly shaped ZIKV transmission, whereas ZIKV had little effect on DENV transmission. Under cross-protection, ZIKV transmission occurred as infrequent outbreaks without changes in long-term ZIKV burden. When prior DENV infection enhanced both ZIKV infection and symptomatic risk, this burden roughly doubled. Population ageing raised the mean age of DENV and ZIKV cases by over 20 years, more than cross-reactivity did. Simulated ZIKV case ages were closer to those reported in Thailand in 2019-2023 under enhancement of ZIKV infection and symptoms. Over half of individuals aged 15-49 years remained ZIKV-naive under all assumptions, leaving many pregnancies at risk of congenital Zika syndrome. Interpreting ZIKV case counts in SEA requires joint consideration of cross-reactivity, demographic change and case detection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) is endemic across Southeast Asia (SEA), where a large proportion of adults have been infected. Prior infection with one virus can protect against or enhance infection with the other, but the population-level consequences are unknown.
Design and population
We developed an age-structured DENV-ZIKV model with nine cross-reactivity assumptions combining cross-protection and subsequent enhancement, and simulated sustained co-circulation under changing Thai demography.
Core methods
Population ageing raised the mean age of DENV and ZIKV cases by over 20 years, more than cross-reactivity did. Simulated ZIKV case ages were closer to those reported in Thailand in 2019-2023 under enhancement of ZIKV infection and symptoms.
Main results
Through cross-reactivity, DENV strongly shaped ZIKV transmission, whereas ZIKV had little effect on DENV transmission. Under cross-protection, ZIKV transmission occurred as infrequent outbreaks without changes in long-term ZIKV burden. When prior DENV infection enhanced both ZIKV infection and symptomatic risk, this burden roughly doubled.
Interpretation and novelty
Interpreting ZIKV case counts in SEA requires joint consideration of cross-reactivity, demographic change and case detection. Over half of individuals aged 15-49 years remained ZIKV-naive under all assumptions, leaving many pregnancies at risk of congenital Zika syndrome.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of considering cross-reactivity and demographic changes when interpreting ZIKV case counts in Southeast Asia. They also underscore the public health risk of congenital Zika syndrome in pregnancies where the mother remains ZIKV-naive.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations. The confidence is high based on the model's assumptions and simulation results.

唐格朗县 登革热 传播的 SIR 模型和稳定性分析

An Analysis of the SIR Model and Stability of Dengue Fever Spread in Tangerang Regency
作者： Sri Wahyuni, Dian Candra Rini Novitasari, Ratna Cintya Dewi, Fajar Setiawan, Rahmat Prasetyadi Widyasmara Nurhadi
摘要中文翻译请注意印度尼西亚登革热 (DBD) 的情况。Demam Berdarah 登革热预防措施是在印度尼西亚的丹格朗卡布帕滕 (Kabupaten Tangerang) 地区进行的。Dalam mengatasi penyebaran penyakit ini,分析模型matematika SIR dapat digunakan untuk pemahaman dan memprediksi dinamika penyebaran penyakit Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD)。Jenis Penelitian 对此进行了研究并对其进行了限制。在该模型中,Demam Berdarah 登革热 (DBD) 模型可用于 SIR 模型、分析、动态分析、动态分析和数字模型模拟 SIR 模型的计算。Hasil penelitian ini menunjukkan bahwa model matematika SIR efektif dalam memodelkan dan menganalisis kesatbilan penyebaran penyakit penyakit Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) di Kabupaten Tangerang. Penelitian 是会员Konstribusi penting dalam menanggulangi dan mengetahui kestabilan penyebaran penyakit。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在使用 SIR 模型了解 登革热 在坦格朗县传播的动态,因为 登革热 在印度尼西亚是一种危险且可能致命的疾病。
设计与对象
该研究是一项定性研究,使用 SIR 模型来分析 登革热 在坦格朗县的传播情况。
核心方法
利用SIR模型对坦格朗县登革热传播的稳定性进行了模拟和分析。
主要结果
研究发现,SIR模型有效地模拟和分析了坦格朗县登革热传播的稳定性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,SIR 模型是理解和预测 登革热 传播动态的有用工具。
科研与公卫意义
该研究有助于了解如何管理和稳定 登革热 在唐格朗县的传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于对数学模型的依赖,没有提供直接的经验数据,因此证据强度中等。

An Analysis of the SIR Model and Stability of Dengue Fever Spread in Tangerang Regency

Authors: Sri Wahyuni, Dian Candra Rini Novitasari, Ratna Cintya Dewi, Fajar Setiawan, Rahmat Prasetyadi Widyasmara Nurhadi
Original abstract (English)
Penyakit yang berbahaya dan bisa menyebabkan kematian jika tidak ditangani dengan tepat di Indonesia salah satunya Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD). Demam Berdarah Dengue menjadi penyakit yang saat ini berkembang di dunia dan masih menjadi masalah kebanyakan masyarakat di Indonesia khususnya di Kabupaten Tangerang. Dalam mengatasi penyebaran penyakit ini, analisis model matematika SIR dapat digunakan untuk pemahaman dan memprediksi dinamika penyebaran penyakit Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD). Jenis penelitian ini adalah studi kepustakaan dan penelitian kuantitatif. Tujuan penelitian ini untuk mengetahui model penyebaran penyakit Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) menggunakan model SIR, analisis dinamik, titik ekuilibrium, kestabilan titik ekuilibrium dan simulasi numerik model matematika SIR pada penyebaran penyakit Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) di Kabupaten Tangerang. Hasil penelitian ini menunjukkan bahwa model matematika SIR efektif dalam memodelkan dan menganalisis kesatbilan penyebaran penyakit penyakit Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) di Kabupaten Tangerang. Penelitian ini memberikan konstribusi penting dalam menanggulangi dan mengetahui kestabilan penyebaran penyakit.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the dynamics of Dengue Fever spread in Tangerang Regency using the SIR model, as Dengue Fever is a dangerous and potentially fatal disease in Indonesia.
Design and population
The research was a qualitative study using the SIR model to analyze the spread of Dengue Fever in Tangerang Regency.
Core methods
The SIR model was used to simulate and analyze the stability of Dengue Fever spread in Tangerang Regency.
Main results
The study found that the SIR model effectively models and analyzes the stability of Dengue Fever spread in Tangerang Regency.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the SIR model is a useful tool for understanding and predicting the dynamics of Dengue Fever spread.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study contributes to the understanding of how to manage and stabilize the spread of Dengue Fever in Tangerang Regency.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a mathematical model and does not provide direct empirical data, thus the evidence strength is moderate.

蚊媒病毒感染及其肌肉骨骼表现:登革热、基孔肯雅热与寨卡病毒的鉴别诊断依据

Arboviroses e suas manifestações musculoesqueléticas subsídios para o diagnóstico diferencial entre dengue, Chikungunya e Zika
作者： Ravel Janguiê Porto Barros, Mateus Dornelas Mitchell Paiva De Almeida, Mateus Alves Garrido, Gabriela van der Linden Mota, Pedro Pereira Medeiros de Mendonça, Renata Cristina Santos de Azevedo, Victoria Lima Macedo, Maria Helena Queiroz de Araújo Mariano
摘要中文翻译引言:登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡热均由埃及伊蚊传播,在巴西同时流行,且具有相似的初期表现,这给鉴别诊断带来困难。目标:描述登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡热的临床特征,特别是肌肉骨骼和全身症状,并系统化鉴别诊断要素。方法:通过SciELO、LILACS和PubMed/MEDLINE数据库进行叙述性综述,时间范围为2012年至2026年,同时结合卫生部文件和巴西风湿病学会的建议。结果:登革热表现为高热持续2至7天,伴有剧烈肌肉疼痛和轻度关节痛,退热期有血浆渗漏的风险。基孔肯雅热则超过90%的患者出现对称性且致残的多关节痛,伴有水肿和腱鞘炎,约有一半患者发展为慢性疾病,有时表现为侵蚀性关节炎。寨卡热则以低热或无热为主,早期出现瘙痒性皮疹,常见结膜炎,关节痛较轻;先天性感染可能导致关节僵硬。血小板减少提示登革热,淋巴细胞减少提示基孔肯雅热。RT-PCR适用于登革热诊断,建议在发病后第五天内进行,而血清学检测则从第六天开始,且存在黄病毒属之间的交叉反应。在排除登革热之前,应避免使用抗炎药和糖皮质激素。结论:肌肉骨骼表现模式,结合发热和皮疹的发展情况,有助于早期鉴别诊断;基孔肯雅热的慢性化需要结构化的随访和根据疾病阶段进行疼痛管理。科学与公共卫生意义:这些发现可为登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡热的诊断、治疗和监测提供依据,特别是在早期阶段,强调区分这些蚊媒病毒的重要性,以防止误诊和不当治疗。局限性与证据强度:本研究依赖叙述性综述,缺乏随机对照试验,这可能影响证据的强度。证据等级为中等。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述登革热、寨卡热和基孔肯雅热的临床表现,特别关注肌肉骨骼和全身症状,并系统化用于鉴别诊断的要素。科学/公共卫生问题在于,由于这些虫媒病毒病的初始表现重叠,鉴别诊断存在困难。
设计与对象
通过SciELO、LILACS和PubMed/MEDLINE数据库从2012年至2026年进行了叙述性综述,同时补充了卫生部文件和巴西风湿病学会的建议。
核心方法
该综述包括登革热病毒的RT-PCR检测直至第五天,第六天起使用血清学,且存在黄病毒属之间的交叉反应。建议在排除登革热之前避免使用抗炎药物和糖皮质激素。
主要结果
Dengue presents with high fever for two to seven days, intense myalgia, and mild arthralgia, with risk of plasma extravasation during defervescence. Chikungunya is characterized by more than 90% of patients with symmetrical and incapacitating polyarthralgia, edema, and tenosynovitis, with about half evolving to chronic disease, sometimes with erosive arthritis. Zika has low or absent fever, pruriginous exanthem, frequent conjunctivitis, and mild arthralgia; congenital infection may result in arthrogryposis. Trombocytopenia suggests dengue, and lymphopenia suggests Chikungunya.
解释与创新
该研究认为,与发热和皮疹行为相关的肌肉骨骼模式指导了早期的诊断鉴别诊断;慢性寨卡病毒需要结构化的随访和根据疾病阶段进行的疼痛管理。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为这些虫媒病毒的监测、诊断和治疗提供信息,尤其是在早期阶段,并强调区分它们的重要性,以防止误诊和不适当的治疗。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于其依赖于叙述性综述以及缺乏随机对照试验,这可能影响证据的强度。证据等级为中等。

Arboviroses e suas manifestações musculoesqueléticas subsídios para o diagnóstico diferencial entre dengue, Chikungunya e Zika

Authors: Ravel Janguiê Porto Barros, Mateus Dornelas Mitchell Paiva De Almeida, Mateus Alves Garrido, Gabriela van der Linden Mota, Pedro Pereira Medeiros de Mendonça, Renata Cristina Santos de Azevedo, Victoria Lima Macedo, Maria Helena Queiroz de Araújo Mariano
Original abstract (English)
Introdução: Dengue, Chikungunya e Zika, transmitidas pelo Aedes aegypti, cocirculam no Brasil e compartilham manifestações iniciais, o que dificulta o diagnóstico diferencial.Objetivo: Caracterizar o quadro clínico de dengue, Chikungunya e Zika, com ênfase nos sintomas musculoesqueléticos e constitucionais, e sistematizar elementos para o diagnóstico diferencial. Métodos: Revisão narrativa nas bases SciELO, LILACS e PubMed/MEDLINE, com publicações de 2012 a 2026, complementada por documentos do Ministério da Saúde e recomendações da Sociedade Brasileira de Reumatologia. Resultados: Na dengue, predominam febre alta por dois a sete dias, mialgia intensa e artralgia leve, com risco de extravasamento plasmático na defervescência. Na Chikungunya, mais de 90% dos pacientes apresentam poliatralgia simétrica e incapacitante, com edema e tenossinovite, e cerca de metade evolui para doença crônica, por vezes com artrite erosiva. Na Zika, a febre é baixa ou ausente, o exantema pruriginoso é precoce, a conjuntivite é frequente e a artralgia é discreta; a infecção congênita pode cursar com artrogripose. Trombocitopenia sugere dengue, e linfopenia, Chikungunya. O RT-PCR é indicado é indicado até o quinto dia e a sorologia a partir do sexto, com reação cruzada entre flavivírus. Anti-inflamatórios e corticosteroides devem ser evitados até que a dengue seja afastada. Conclusão: O padrão musculoesquelético, associado ao comportamento da febre e do exantema, orienta o diagnóstico diferencial nos primeiros dias; a cronificação da Chikungunya exige seguimento estruturado e manejo da dor conforme a fase da doença.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the clinical picture of dengue, Chikungunya, and Zika, with emphasis on musculoskeletal and constitutional symptoms, and to systematize elements for differential diagnosis. The scientific/public-health problem is the difficulty in differentiating these arboviroses due to their overlapping initial manifestations.
Design and population
A narrative review was conducted using SciELO, LILACS, and PubMed/MEDLINE databases from 2012 to 2026, supplemented by Ministry of Health documents and recommendations from the Brazilian Society of Rheumatology.
Core methods
The review included RT-PCR for dengue until the fifth day and serology from the sixth day, with cross-reactivity between flaviviruses. Anti-inflammatory and corticosteroid use was advised to be avoided until dengue was ruled out.
Main results
Dengue presents with high fever for two to seven days, intense myalgia, and mild arthralgia, with risk of plasma extravasation during defervescence. Chikungunya is characterized by more than 90% of patients with symmetrical and incapacitating polyarthralgia, edema, and tenosynovitis, with about half evolving to chronic disease, sometimes with erosive arthritis. Zika has low or absent fever, pruriginous exanthem, frequent conjunctivitis, and mild arthralgia; congenital infection may result in arthrogryposis. Trombocytopenia suggests dengue, and lymphopenia suggests Chikungunya.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interpreted that the musculoskeletal pattern, associated with fever and exanthem behavior, guides differential diagnosis in the early days; chronic Chikungunya requires structured follow-up and pain management according to the disease phase.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of these arboviroses, particularly in the early stages, and highlight the importance of distinguishing between them to prevent misdiagnosis and inappropriate treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on narrative review and the absence of randomized controlled trials, which may affect the strength of the evidence. The evidence is moderate.

登革热不止于大城市:巴西一个小型市镇12年的历史系列研究

DENGUE ALÉM DOS GRANDES CENTROS: SÉRIE HISTÓRICA DE 12 ANOS EM UM MUNICÍPIO BRASILEIRO DE PEQUENO PORTE
作者： Ewerton Teixeira Lopes, Gustavo Peixoto Chaves, Leandro Bernardes Barreto, Luan Pinheiro Guimarães, Yago Rayth do Carmo, Zuinara Pereira Gusmão Maia
摘要中文翻译登革热是巴西重要的公共卫生问题,由于其高度的传播能力、疫情反复、医疗负担以及环境、社会和气候因素的影响。本研究分析了2014年至2025年间巴西米纳斯吉拉斯州Padre Paraíso市镇的登革热流行病学和地方性特征,并与州级情况进行了比较。这是一项基于历史数据的生态学研究,采用定量分析方法,数据来源于SINAN/TABNET/DATASUS和IBGE。研究分析了流行病学指标、社会人口学信息、临床结局以及登革病毒血清型的循环情况。地方性通过基于中位数和四分位数的发病率中位数和四分位数计算的流行病学通道进行评估,时间范围为2014年至2025年。该市镇的疫情周期与州级情况同步,但发病率比例更高,且在流行病学周8至25之间呈现出反复的季节性模式。观察到病例主要集中在女性、20至59岁年龄组以及混血种人中。该市镇未记录登革热死亡病例,而米纳斯吉拉斯州每年均有登革热死亡病例。

DENGUE ALÉM DOS GRANDES CENTROS: SÉRIE HISTÓRICA DE 12 ANOS EM UM MUNICÍPIO BRASILEIRO DE PEQUENO PORTE

Authors: Ewerton Teixeira Lopes, Gustavo Peixoto Chaves, Leandro Bernardes Barreto, Luan Pinheiro Guimarães, Yago Rayth do Carmo, Zuinara Pereira Gusmão Maia
Original abstract (English)
A dengue constitui importante problema de saúde pública no Brasil, devido à sua elevada capacidade de disseminação, recorrência epidêmica, impacto assistencial e influência de fatores ambientais, sociais e climáticos. Este estudo analisou o perfil epidemiológico e endêmico da dengue no município de Padre Paraíso, Minas Gerais, entre 2014 e 2025, em comparação com o cenário estadual. Trata-se de um estudo ecológico de série histórica, com abordagem quantitativa, realizado a partir de dados do SINAN/TABNET/DATASUS e do IBGE. Foram analisados indicadores epidemiológicos, informações sociodemográficas, desfechos clínicos e circulação sorotípica do vírus da dengue. A endemicidade foi avaliada por meio de canal endêmico baseado em mediana e quartis dos coeficientes de incidência por semana epidemiológica considerando o período 2014 a 2025. O município apresentou ciclos epidêmicos sincronizados ao cenário estadual, porém com maior intensidade proporcional e padrão sazonal recorrente entre as semanas epidemiológicas 8 e 25. Observou-se prevalência de casos para o sexo feminino, faixa etária de 20 a 59 anos e em indivíduos pardos. Não houve registro de óbitos por dengue no município, enquanto Minas Gerais apresentou óbitos em todos os anos.

孟加拉国全国范围内对基于区级信息的自下而上方法和直接国家级方法在一周期前登革热住院病例预测中的评估

Nationwide evaluation of district-informed bottom-up and direct national approaches for one-week-ahead forecasting of dengue hospital admissions in Bangladesh
作者： Muhammad Ziaul Haque, Md. Razu Ahmed, Mimosa Saha, Md. Feroz Ali, Diganto Biswas, Md Shafiul Alam, S M Shakil, Obaidullah Obaidi
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热对孟加拉国的监测和医院服务造成持续压力,但大多数以孟加拉国为中心的预测研究都使用了国家级或时间聚合的数据。在进行国家级聚合之前保留区级信息是否能为短期预测提供额外价值仍不确定。本研究评估了基于区级信息和直接国家级方法在孟加拉国所有64个区的一周期前登革热住院病例预测。2022-2025年的全国监测-气象学面板包含93,504个区级日观测数据,汇总为13,312个完整的区级周记录。病例报告按报告区组织,而非患者居住地。一个单一的区级周数据池Light Gradient Boosting Machine(LightGBM)生成了区级特定的预测,并将这些预测汇总以获得国家级预测。该自下而上的方法与直接国家级LightGBM、负二项广义可加模型、一周期持续预测以及分层预测基准进行了比较。模型开发按时间顺序进行:2024年用于验证和模型选择,最终的模型规格使用所有2025年前的模型可用数据重新拟合,模型在整个2025年52周的评估期间保持不变。由于在初步2025年结果已知后,模型规格在同行评审过程中进行了修订,因此2025年未被纳入模型拟合,但不被视为完全未受干扰的测试集。自下而上的LightGBM在2025年实现了最低的国家级点误差(MAE = 239.37;RMSE = 380.27;wMAPE = 12.10%;R2 = 0.9625),与直接国家级负二项广义可加模型(wMAPE = 14.85%)、一周期持续预测(15.65%)和直接国家级LightGBM(18.67%)相比。在区级周层面,与持续预测相比的改进较为有限(wMAPE = 28.34% vs. 15.65%)。三个主要的全国wMAPE差异的95%圆块自举置信区间均跨越了零点,调整后的预测准确性测试未能确立统计上的优势。分层协调并未将国家级点误差降低到未协调的自下而上预测之下。SHapley Additive exPlanations(SHAP)识别出近期病例是主要的预测信号,而进一步分析表明,预测误差的大小在地理上存在异质性。基于残差的实证95%不确定性区间覆盖了52个未被使用的周中的49个(94.23%)。基于区级信息的自下而上预测实现了最低的观察到的国家级点误差,同时保留了次国家级预测信息,但其相对于强比较对象的优势仍存在统计不确定性。在常规早期预警使用之前,需要进行前瞻性多季节验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热对孟加拉国的监测和医院服务造成持续压力,但大多数以孟加拉国为中心的预测研究都使用了国家级或时间聚合的数据。
设计与对象
本研究评估了基于区级信息和直接国家级方法在孟加拉国所有64个区的一周期前登革热住院病例预测。
核心方法
一个单一的区级周数据池Light Gradient Boosting Machine(LightGBM)生成了区级特定的预测,并将这些预测汇总以获得国家级预测。本研究采用前瞻性多季节验证方法,其中2024年用于验证和模型选择,最终的模型规格使用所有2025年前的模型可用数据重新拟合。
主要结果
自下而上的LightGBM在2025年实现了最低的观察到的国家级点误差(MAE = 239.37;RMSE = 380.27;wMAPE = 12.10%;R2 = 0.9625),与直接国家级负二项广义可加模型(wMAPE = 14.85%)、一周期持续预测(15.65%)和直接国家级LightGBM(18.67%)相比。
解释与创新
基于区级信息的自下而上预测实现了最低的观察到的国家级点误差,同时保留了次国家级预测信息,但其相对于强比较对象的优势仍存在统计不确定性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在进行国家级聚合之前保留区级信息可能为登革热住院病例的短期预测提供额外价值,这可能有助于制定更具针对性的公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
本研究的设计局限性包括:在初步2025年结果已知后,模型规格在同行评审过程中进行了修订;主要的国家级wMAPE差异的95%圆块自举置信区间均跨越了零点,表明存在统计不确定性。

Nationwide evaluation of district-informed bottom-up and direct national approaches for one-week-ahead forecasting of dengue hospital admissions in Bangladesh

Authors: Muhammad Ziaul Haque, Md. Razu Ahmed, Mimosa Saha, Md. Feroz Ali, Diganto Biswas, Md Shafiul Alam, S M Shakil, Obaidullah Obaidi
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue places recurrent pressure on surveillance and hospital services in Bangladesh, yet most Bangladesh-focused forecasting studies have used national or temporally aggregated data. Whether retaining district-level information before national aggregation provides additional value for short-range forecasting remains uncertain. This study evaluated district-informed and direct national approaches for one-week-ahead forecasting of reported dengue hospital admissions across all 64 districts of Bangladesh. A nationwide surveillance–meteorological panel for 2022–2025 comprised 93,504 district-day observations aggregated into 13,312 complete district-week records. Admissions were organized by reporting district, not patient residence. A single pooled district-week Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LightGBM) generated district-specific forecasts that were summed to obtain national predictions. This bottom-up approach was compared with direct national LightGBM, a negative binomial generalized additive model, one-week persistence, and hierarchical forecasting benchmarks. Model development was temporally ordered: 2024 was used for validation and model selection, finalized specifications were refitted using all pre-2025 model-ready data, and models remained fixed throughout the 52-week 2025 evaluation. Because model specifications were revised during peer review after initial 2025 results were known, 2025 was held out from model fitting but is not treated as an entirely untouched test set. Bottom-up LightGBM achieved the lowest observed national point error in 2025 (MAE = 239.37; RMSE = 380.27; wMAPE = 12.10%; R2 = 0.9625), compared with the direct national negative binomial generalized additive model (wMAPE = 14.85%), one-week persistence (15.65%), and direct national LightGBM (18.67%). At district-week level, improvement over persistence was modest (wMAPE = 28.34% vs. All three primary 95% circular block-bootstrap confidence intervals for the primary national wMAPE differences crossed zero, and adjusted predictive-accuracy tests did not establish statistical superiority. Hierarchical reconciliation did not reduce national point error below the unreconciled bottom-up forecast. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) identified recent admissions as the dominant predictive signal, while additional analyses showed geographic heterogeneity in forecast-error magnitude. The empirical 95% residual-based uncertainty interval covered 49 of 52 held-out weeks (94.23%). District-informed bottom-up forecasting achieved the lowest observed national point error while retaining subnational forecast information, but its advantage over strong comparators remained statistically uncertain. Prospective multi-season validation is required before routine early-warning use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue places recurrent pressure on surveillance and hospital services in Bangladesh, yet most Bangladesh-focused forecasting studies have used national or temporally aggregated data.
Design and population
This study evaluated district-informed and direct national approaches for one-week-ahead forecasting of reported dengue hospital admissions across all 64 districts of Bangladesh.
Core methods
A single pooled district-week Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LightGBM) generated district-specific forecasts that were summed to obtain national predictions. The study used a prospective multi-season validation approach, with 2024 for validation and model selection, and finalized specifications refitted using all pre-2025 model-ready data.
Main results
Bottom-up LightGBM achieved the lowest observed national point error in 2025 (MAE = 239.37; RMSE = 380.27; wMAPE = 12.10%; R2 = 0.9625), compared with the direct national negative binomial generalized additive model (wMAPE = 14.85%), one-week persistence (15.65%), and direct national LightGBM (18.67%).
Interpretation and novelty
District-informed bottom-up forecasting achieved the lowest observed national point error while retaining subnational forecast information, but its advantage over strong comparators remained statistically uncertain.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that retaining district-level information before national aggregation may provide additional value for short-range forecasting of dengue hospital admissions, which could inform more targeted public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations include the fact that model specifications were revised during peer review after initial 2025 results were known, and the confidence intervals for the primary national wMAPE differences crossed zero, indicating statistical uncertainty.

登革热疾病传播模型的全局特性及最优控制研究

A study of the global properties and optimal control of dengue disease transmission model
作者： Harouna Ouedraogo, Dramane Ouedraogo, I. Ibrango, A. Guiro
摘要中文翻译在这项工作中,我们制定了一个非线性微分方程组,对 登革热 的 传播 动力学进行建模。该模型表现出经典的阈值行为。我们证明,当基本再生数小于 1 时,无病平衡局部和全局渐近稳定;当基本再生数大于 1 时,地方性平衡在一定条件下全局渐近稳定。平衡的稳定性是通过利用李雅普诺夫稳定性理论和拉萨尔不变原理推导出来的。此外,我们还制定了一个最优控制问题,涉及预防措施、易感人群疫苗接种的投资以及与杀虫剂治疗相关的投资。提供数值模拟来说明理论结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过非线性微分方程模型模拟登革热的传播动力学,并通过非线性微分方程系统理解其全球特性。该研究针对改进数学框架以分析登革热传播和控制策略的需求进行了探讨。
设计与对象
本研究涉及建立一个数学模型以模拟登革热传播动力学。该研究在理论环境中进行,使用微分方程,专注于登革热及其相关因素,而没有具体参考现实世界的人群或数据。
核心方法
该模型通过非线性微分方程系统进行构建,利用李雅普诺夫稳定性理论和拉塞尔不变性原理分析平衡点的稳定性。研究还包括构建一个最优控制问题以评估登革热的预防措施和干预手段。
主要结果
该模型展示了经典的阈值行为,当基本再生数小于1时,无病平衡点在局部和全局上都是渐近稳定的。当基本再生数超过1时,在某些条件下,地方性平衡点在全局上是渐近稳定的。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为模型能够基于基本再生数预测登革热动力学的证据。其新颖之处在于将最优控制理论应用于评估登革热传播的干预措施。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为登革热监测的数学模型开发以及评估控制策略(如疫苗接种和杀虫剂治疗)提供信息。它们为理解疾病传播和干预措施的有效性提供了理论基础。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于理论建模,未包含实证数据或现实世界验证。证据来源于数学分析和模拟,可能无法完全反映登革热传播在真实人群中的复杂性。由于证据仅基于摘要,结果的可信度中等。

A study of the global properties and optimal control of dengue disease transmission model

Authors: Harouna Ouedraogo, Dramane Ouedraogo, I. Ibrango, A. Guiro
Original abstract (English)
In this work, we formulate a nonlinear system of differential equations that models the transmission dynamics of dengue fever. The model exhibits the classical threshold behavior. We prove that when the basic reproduction number is less than one, the disease-free equilibrium is locally and globally asymptotically stable and when the basic reproduction number is greater than one the endemic equilibrium is globally asymptotically stable under certain conditions. The stability of equilibria is derived through the use of Lyapunov stability theory and LaSalle's invariance principle. In addition we formulate an optimal control problem involving preventive measures, the investment in vaccination of susceptible humans and the investment related to insecticide treatment. Numerical simulations are provided to illustrate the theoretical results.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to model the transmission dynamics of dengue fever and understand its global properties through a nonlinear system of differential equations. The research addresses the need for improved mathematical frameworks to analyze dengue disease spread and control strategies.
Design and population
The study involved the formulation of a mathematical model to simulate dengue disease transmission dynamics. It was conducted in a theoretical setting using differential equations, focusing on dengue fever and its associated factors without specific reference to real-world populations or data.
Core methods
The model was developed using a nonlinear system of differential equations, and the stability of equilibria was analyzed through Lyapunov stability theory and LaSalle's invariance principle. The study also included the formulation of an optimal control problem to evaluate preventive measures and interventions.
Main results
The model demonstrated classical threshold behavior, with the disease-free equilibrium being locally and globally asymptotically stable when the basic reproduction number is less than one. When the basic reproduction number exceeds one, the endemic equilibrium is globally asymptotically stable under certain conditions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as evidence of the model's ability to predict dengue disease dynamics based on the basic reproduction number. The novelty lies in the application of optimal control theory to evaluate interventions for dengue disease transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of mathematical models for dengue disease surveillance and the evaluation of control strategies such as vaccination and insecticide treatment. They provide a theoretical basis for understanding disease spread and intervention effectiveness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to theoretical modeling and does not include empirical data or real-world validation. The evidence is derived from mathematical analysis and simulations, which may not fully capture the complexities of dengue disease transmission in real populations. The confidence in the results is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the evidence.

High genomic diversity of dengue virus strains in Latin America and Asia-Pacific: insights from a phase 2 mosnodenvir clinical trial

High genomic diversity of dengue virus strains in Latin America and Asia-Pacific: insights from a phase 2 mosnodenvir clinical trial
作者： Veerle Van Eygen, Leen Vijgen, Samuel L. Hong, Annemie Buelens, Sandra De Meyer, Marnix Van Loock, Philippe Lemey, Freya Rasschaert, Guillermo Herrera-Taracena, Liesbeth Van Wesenbeeck
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管在疾病监测方面取得了进展,但登革病毒(DENV)毒株的基因组监测仍有限且局限于特定地区。本研究旨在评估拉丁美洲和亚太地区当前传播的DENV基因组多样性。
设计与对象
A Phase 2 clinical trial (NCT05201794) of mosnodenvir prophylaxis was conducted in endemic regions of Asia-Pacific and Latin America between 2023 and 2024. The study involved 411 dengue index cases (ICs) across 30 sites in 7 countries.
核心方法
通过RT-PCR确定DENV血清型,并对7个国家30个地点中代表随时间观察到的DENV血清型的150例ICs进行了全基因组测序。
主要结果
在拉丁美洲和亚太地区均观察到所有四种DENV血清型的共循环现象。DENV-1是最主要的血清型(51.1%),其基因型分布存在显著的地理差异。研究发现2023–2024年间拉丁美洲地区DENV-3和DENV-4毒株的传播增加。
解释与创新
本研究捕捉了2023–2024年间拉丁美洲和亚太地区广泛存在的当代DENV多样性,突出了DENV 进化的动态特性及其传播模式。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了基因组监测对于公共卫生准备的重要性,以减轻登革热风险,特别是在那些频繁出现病毒(再)引入的地区,这可能会引发新的疫情。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其关注的是临床试验人群,这可能无法完全代表一般人群中DENV的传播情况。由于数据的观察性质,证据强度中等。

High genomic diversity of dengue virus strains in Latin America and Asia-Pacific: insights from a phase 2 mosnodenvir clinical trial

Authors: Veerle Van Eygen, Leen Vijgen, Samuel L. Hong, Annemie Buelens, Sandra De Meyer, Marnix Van Loock, Philippe Lemey, Freya Rasschaert, Guillermo Herrera-Taracena, Liesbeth Van Wesenbeeck
Original abstract (English)
The study involved 411 dengue index cases (ICs). We identified increased circulation of DENV-3 and DENV-4 strains in Latin America during 2023–2024. Time-resolved phylogenetic analyses show that the study captured a broad cross-section of contemporary DENV diversity circulating across Latin America and the Asia-Pacific region during 2023–2024. Our findings stress the importance of genomic surveillance for public health preparedness to mitigate dengue risk, highlighting a mosaic of local lineages and frequent virus (re)-introductions, which may trigger new epidemics. Acknowledgements We thank all study participants, without whom this research would not have been possible and whose collaboration contributed to the advancement of health research in the field of tropical diseases. We thank all principal investigators and sites for their contribution to this study. Abstract Despite advances in disease monitoring, genomic surveillance of dengue virus (DENV) strains remains limited and regionally confined. The Phase 2 clinical trial (NCT05201794) of mosnodenvir prophylaxis conducted in endemic regions of Asia-Pacific and Latin America (2023–2024) provided a unique resource to assess genomic diversity of circulating DENVs. DENV serotypes were determined using RT-PCR. Whole-genome sequencing was conducted for a subset of ICs (n = 150) representing DENV serotypes observed over time at 30 sites across 7 countries. Cocirculation of all four DENV serotypes was observed in both Latin America and Asia-Pacific regions. Overall, DENV-1 was the predominant serotype (51.1%), with significant geographical variations in genotype distribution: DENV-1 genotype V in Latin America versus genotypes I and IV in the Asia-Pacific region. The authors would like to thank Anne-Theres Henze for her me...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Genomic surveillance of dengue virus (DENV) strains remains limited and regionally confined, despite advances in disease monitoring. This study aimed to assess the genomic diversity of circulating DENVs in Latin America and Asia-Pacific regions.
Design and population
A Phase 2 clinical trial (NCT05201794) of mosnodenvir prophylaxis was conducted in endemic regions of Asia-Pacific and Latin America between 2023 and 2024. The study involved 411 dengue index cases (ICs) across 30 sites in 7 countries.
Core methods
DENV serotypes were determined using RT-PCR, and whole-genome sequencing was conducted for a subset of ICs (n = 150) representing DENV serotypes observed over time at 30 sites across 7 countries.
Main results
Cocirculation of all four DENV serotypes was observed in both Latin America and Asia-Pacific regions. DENV-1 was the predominant serotype (51.1%), with significant geographical variations in genotype distribution. Increased circulation of DENV-3 and DENV-4 strains was identified in Latin America during 2023–2024.
Interpretation and novelty
The study captured a broad cross-section of contemporary DENV diversity circulating across Latin America and the Asia-Pacific region during 2023–2024, highlighting the dynamic nature of DENV evolution and spread.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for genomic surveillance to inform public health preparedness and mitigate dengue risk, particularly in regions with frequent virus (re)-introductions that may trigger new epidemics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a clinical trial population, which may not fully represent the broader DENV circulation in the general population. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the data.

加纳发热患者中的登革热:患病率和设施层面的差异:一项多地点监测研究

Dengue among febrile patients in Ghana: prevalence and facility-level variation in a multisite surveillance study
作者： Francisca Zigah, Adwoa Afrakoma Agyei-Nkansah, Emmanuel Ayitey Tagoe, Ivanda A. T. Gyapon, Bonney, K., Adomako-Boateng, F., Damien Punguyire, Joseph A. Oliver-Commey, Richard Socrate Adzesi, Kwamena William Coleman Sagoe
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在加纳,登革热被低估,且实验室检测有限。我们估计了发热患者中登革热NS1和/或IgM阳性率,并描述了地理、时间及人口学差异。
设计与对象
我们在2023年8月至2025年7月期间,在加纳首都地区、博诺东区和上西区的六家公共医疗机构中,对同意参与的年龄≥1年的发热患者进行了横断面监测。我们随机选择每个生态区域中的一个地区,并选择发热病例数量较多的医疗机构。我们接触并招募了连续符合条件的患者。
核心方法
我们通过ELISA检测血清中的登革热NS1抗原和IgM抗体;NS1阳性样本进一步进行RT-PCR和血清型鉴定。我们估计了比例及其95%置信区间(CI),并使用逻辑回归分析调整后的关联。
主要结果
Among 2,936 participants, 204 were NS1- and/or IgM-positive (6.95%; 95% CI, 6.05-7.93%): 31 were NS1-only, 161 IgM-only, and 12 dual-positive. Prevalence ranged from 3.88% (95% CI, 2.19-6.31%) at Kintampo to 12.32% (9.56-15.55%) at Atebubu, both in Bono East. Adjusted odds increased with age (aOR per five years, 1.09; 95% CI, 1.05-1.14). Eight of 43 NS1-positive samples contained dengue RNA: five DENV-1 from Bono East and three DENV-3 from Upper West. There was no clear rainy-dry season difference.
解释与创新
在所有六家医疗机构的发热患者中均检测到登革热NS1和/或IgM阳性,RT-PCR证实了DENV-1和DENV-3的流行。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持将登革热纳入发热疾病的评估,并加强加纳实验室支持的监测。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告具体的研究设计或证据强度的局限性。

Dengue among febrile patients in Ghana: prevalence and facility-level variation in a multisite surveillance study

Authors: Francisca Zigah, Adwoa Afrakoma Agyei-Nkansah, Emmanuel Ayitey Tagoe, Ivanda A. T. Gyapon, Bonney, K., Adomako-Boateng, F., Damien Punguyire, Joseph A. Oliver-Commey, Richard Socrate Adzesi, Kwamena William Coleman Sagoe
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Dengue is under-recognized in Ghana, where laboratory testing is limited. We estimated dengue NS1 and/or IgM positivity among febrile patients and described geographical, temporal, and demographic variation. Methodology: We conducted cross-sectional surveillance among consenting febrile patients aged [≥]1 year at six public facilities in Greater Accra, Bono East, and Upper West between August 2023 and July 2025. We randomly selected one region within each of three ecological strata and selected facilities with high febrile-case volumes. We approached and recruited consecutive eligible patients. We tested serum for dengue NS1 antigen and IgM antibody by ELISA; positive NS1 samples underwent RT-PCR and serotyping. We estimated proportions with 95% confidence intervals (CI) and used logistic regression to examine adjusted associations. Results: Among 2,936 participants, 204 were NS1- and/or IgM-positive (6.95%; 95% CI, 6.05-7.93%): 31 were NS1-only, 161 IgM-only, and 12 dual-positive. Prevalence ranged from 3.88% (95% CI, 2.19-6.31%) at Kintampo to 12.32% (9.56-15.55%) at Atebubu, both in Bono East. Adjusted odds increased with age (aOR per five years, 1.09; 95% CI, 1.05-1.14). Eight of 43 NS1-positive samples contained dengue RNA: five DENV-1 from Bono East and three DENV-3 from Upper West. There was no clear rainy-dry season difference. Conclusions: Dengue NS1 and/or IgM were detected among febrile patients at all six facilities, and RT-PCR confirmed circulation of DENV-1 and DENV-3. These findings support including dengue in febrile illness evaluation and strengthening laboratory-supported surveillance in Ghana. Keywords: dengue, febrile illness, NS1 antigen, IgM, DENV-1, DENV-3, Ghana
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is under-recognized in Ghana, where laboratory testing is limited. We estimated dengue NS1 and/or IgM positivity among febrile patients and described geographical, temporal, and demographic variation.
Design and population
We conducted cross-sectional surveillance among consenting febrile patients aged [≥]1 year at six public facilities in Greater Accra, Bono East, and Upper West between August 2023 and July 2025. We randomly selected one region within each of three ecological strata and selected facilities with high febrile-case volumes. We approached and recruited consecutive eligible patients.
Core methods
We tested serum for dengue NS1 antigen and IgM antibody by ELISA; positive NS1 samples underwent RT-PCR and serotyping. We estimated proportions with 95% confidence intervals (CI) and used logistic regression to examine adjusted associations.
Main results
Among 2,936 participants, 204 were NS1- and/or IgM-positive (6.95%; 95% CI, 6.05-7.93%): 31 were NS1-only, 161 IgM-only, and 12 dual-positive. Prevalence ranged from 3.88% (95% CI, 2.19-6.31%) at Kintampo to 12.32% (9.56-15.55%) at Atebubu, both in Bono East. Adjusted odds increased with age (aOR per five years, 1.09; 95% CI, 1.05-1.14). Eight of 43 NS1-positive samples contained dengue RNA: five DENV-1 from Bono East and three DENV-3 from Upper West. There was no clear rainy-dry season difference.
Interpretation and novelty
Dengue NS1 and/or IgM were detected among febrile patients at all six facilities, and RT-PCR confirmed circulation of DENV-1 and DENV-3.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support including dengue in febrile illness evaluation and strengthening laboratory-supported surveillance in Ghana.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations to the study design or evidence strength.

圣多明各省登革热流行病学研究(2021-2025年)

Epidemiología del dengue en Santo Domingo 2021-2025
作者： Maria Guadalupe Guaman Chabla, Jomany Julixa Peralta Perea, Marilyn Janeth Maxi Macías, Evelin Alexandra Zúñiga Sosa
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在分析2021年至2025年间圣多明各德洛斯塔希拉省登革热报告的时序行为,以识别每周和每年的变化。
设计与对象
基于聚合的流行病学记录,进行了一项回顾性、描述性和观察性的生态时间序列研究。
核心方法
数据在Microsoft Excel中按年和流行病学周进行整理,并通过IBM SPSS Statistics 27版进行处理,采用描述性统计方法。
主要结果
共分析了15,744例登革热报告。2023年观察到最高的每周报告集中度,主要集中在流行病学周28至46之间;2024年,最高频率出现在年度的前半段。年度分布显示,2023年有6,312例报告(40.09%),2024年有5,890例(37.41%),两者合计占总数的77.50%。2025年记录了1,385例报告,比2024年减少了76.49%。
解释与创新
这些发现为省级流行病学监测和监测与预防行动的规划提供了描述性参考。建议未来研究中纳入气候、昆虫学、病毒学和社会环境变量。
科研与公卫意义
本研究的结果可为该省登革热预防和控制行动的制定提供依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括其回顾性性质以及依赖聚合数据,这可能限制研究结果的普遍适用性。证据强度为中等。

Epidemiología del dengue en Santo Domingo 2021-2025

Authors: Maria Guadalupe Guaman Chabla, Jomany Julixa Peralta Perea, Marilyn Janeth Maxi Macías, Evelin Alexandra Zúñiga Sosa
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: Analizar el comportamiento temporal de las notificaciones de dengue en la provincia de Santo Domingo de los Tsáchilas durante 2021–2025, a fin de identificar sus variaciones semanales y anuales. Metodología: Se realizó un estudio observacional, descriptivo y retrospectivo, con diseño ecológico de serie temporal, basado en registros epidemiológicos agregados. Los datos se organizaron por año y semana epidemiológica en Microsoft Excel y se procesaron mediante IBM SPSS Statistics, versión 27, utilizando estadística descriptiva. Además, las semanas se agruparon en cuatro bloques operativos: SE 1–13, SE 14–26, SE 27–39 y SE 40–53. Resultados: Se analizaron 15.744 notificaciones de dengue. En 2023 se observó la mayor concentración semanal, principalmente entre las semanas epidemiológicas 28 y 46; en 2024, las frecuencias más elevadas se registraron durante la primera mitad del año. La distribución anual mostró que 2023 concentró 6.312 notificaciones (40,09 %) y 2024, 5.890 (37,41 %), que representaron conjuntamente el 77,50 % del total. En 2025 se registraron 1.385 notificaciones, cifra que representó una reducción del 76,49 % respecto de 2024. Conclusiones: Las notificaciones presentaron variaciones temporales importantes, con una mayor concentración en 2023 y 2024. Los hallazgos aportaron una referencia descriptiva para el seguimiento epidemiológico provincial y la planificación de acciones de vigilancia y prevención. Se recomendó incorporar variables climáticas, entomológicas, virológicas y socioambientales en futuras investigaciones.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to analyze the temporal behavior of dengue notifications in the province of Santo Domingo de los Tsáchilas from 2021 to 2025 to identify weekly and annual variations.
Design and population
A retrospective, descriptive, and observational ecological time series study was conducted based on aggregated epidemiological records.
Core methods
Data were organized by year and epidemiological week in Microsoft Excel and processed using IBM SPSS Statistics, version 27, with descriptive statistics.
Main results
A total of 15,744 dengue notifications were analyzed. The highest weekly concentration was observed in 2023, mainly between epidemiological weeks 28 and 46; in 2024, the highest frequencies were recorded during the first half of the year. The annual distribution showed that 2023 had 6,312 notifications (40.09%) and 2024, 5,890 (37.41%), which together represented 77.50% of the total. In 2025, 1,385 notifications were recorded, a 76.49% reduction from 2024.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provided a descriptive reference for provincial epidemiological monitoring and the planning of surveillance and prevention actions. It was recommended to incorporate climatic, entomological, virological, and socio-environmental variables in future research.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study's findings can inform the surveillance and planning of actions for dengue prevention and control in the province.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the retrospective nature and the reliance on aggregated data, which may limit the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate.

基础模型捕捉人类和环境背景以提高登革热预测能力

Foundation models capture human and environmental context to improve dengue forecasting
作者： John‐Stuart Brittain, Cathal L. Mills, Ciara Judge, Leonardo Soares Bastos, Laís Picinini Freitas, Luiz Max Carvalho, Eduardo Corrêa Araújo, Flávio Codeço Coelho, Yanhua Xu, Benjamin Reddy
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
准确且及时的登革热概率预测可以支持公共卫生准备,但要在不同地点和时间范围内产生可靠的预测仍面临挑战。
设计与对象
我们评估了五种统计、机器学习、深度学习和基础模型方法,用于在巴西5,570个市镇范围内预测每月报告的登革热病例,预测时间范围包括1个月、3个月、6个月和12个月,并从2020年1月至2025年8月进行回溯验证。
核心方法
我们随后测试了从人口动态基础模型(PDFM)中提取的人类和环境背景的空间表示是否能通过后处理残差调整来提高预测效果。
主要结果
时间序列基础模型TimesFM在更长的时间范围内保持了相对稳定的概率预测性能,而大多数其他模型则表现出性能下降。
解释与创新
总体而言,这些发现突显了预训练表示在登革热预测中的潜力,其效益取决于预测架构、时间范围和建模配置。
科研与公卫意义
The largest improvements were observed for N-BEATS, including a reduction in mean WIS of 1.13 at the six-month horizon (95% CI [0.84, 1.48]; Diebold-Mariano p < 0.0001), with improvements also observed for XGBoost.
局限与证据强度
协变量选择、空间拟合和预测聚合策略表现出强烈的模型依赖性效应,而每月模型重新训练在所评估的设置中并未提供一致的优势。

Foundation models capture human and environmental context to improve dengue forecasting

Authors: John‐Stuart Brittain, Cathal L. Mills, Ciara Judge, Leonardo Soares Bastos, Laís Picinini Freitas, Luiz Max Carvalho, Eduardo Corrêa Araújo, Flávio Codeço Coelho, Yanhua Xu, Benjamin Reddy
Original abstract (English)
Accurate and timely probabilistic dengue forecasts can support public health preparedness, but producing reliable forecasts across locations and time horizons remains challenging. We evaluated five statistical, machine-learning, deep-learning, and foundation-model approaches for forecasting monthly reported dengue cases across 5,570 Brazilian municipalities at 1-, 3-, 6- and 12-month horizons, with forecasts backtested from January 2020 to August 2025. The time-series foundation model TimesFM maintained comparatively stable probabilistic forecast performance at longer horizons, while most other models showed declining performance. We then tested whether spatial representations of human and environmental context from a Population Dynamics Foundation Model (PDFM) could improve forecasts through post-hoc residual adjustment. PDFM adjustment improved mean probabilistic forecast performance, measured by the Weighted Interval Score (WIS), in 19 of 20 model-horizon combinations, although the magnitude and frequency of improvements varied across models, horizons and municipalities. The largest improvements were observed for N-BEATS, including a reduction in mean WIS of 1.13 at the six-month horizon (95% CI [0.84, 1.48]; Diebold-Mariano p < 0.0001), with improvements also observed for XGBoost. The proportion of forecasts improved by PDFM generally increased with locally reported dengue case counts, although this relationship varied between models. Covariate selection, spatial fitting and forecast aggregation strategies showed strong model-dependent effects, while monthly model retraining provided no consistent advantage in the settings evaluated. Together, these findings highlight the potential of pretrained representations for dengue forecasting, with benefits dependent on forecasting architecture, horizon and modelling configuration.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Accurate and timely probabilistic dengue forecasts can support public health preparedness, but producing reliable forecasts across locations and time horizons remains challenging.

Core methods

Main results
The time-series foundation model TimesFM maintained comparatively stable probabilistic forecast performance at longer horizons, while most other models showed declining performance.
Interpretation and novelty
Together, these findings highlight the potential of pretrained representations for dengue forecasting, with benefits dependent on forecasting architecture, horizon and modelling configuration.
Scientific and public-health significance
The largest improvements were observed for N-BEATS, including a reduction in mean WIS of 1.13 at the six-month horizon (95% CI [0.84, 1.48]; Diebold-Mariano p < 0.0001), with improvements also observed for XGBoost.
Limitations and evidence strength
Covariate selection, spatial fitting and forecast aggregation strategies showed strong model-dependent effects, while monthly model retraining provided no consistent advantage in the settings evaluated.

圣卡塔琳娜州2021–2025年儿童和青少年登革热的空间与时间分布:为学校健康教育行动提供依据

DISTRIBUIÇÃO ESPACIAL E TEMPORAL DA DENGUE EM CRIANÇAS E ADOLESCENTES EM SANTA CATARINA (2021–2025): SUBSÍDIOS PARA AÇÕES DE EDUCAÇÃO EM SAÚDE NO AMBIENTE ESCOLAR
作者： Charles Junior Finco, Patrícia Poltronieri, Claudia Cristina Marmentini, Maria Assunta Busato
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在分析儿童和青少年登革热疑似病例的空间与时间分布,由于2024年记录了该国最大的登革热疫情,对圣卡塔琳娜州产生了显著影响。
设计与对象
本研究为描述性、定量和回顾性研究,使用来自圣卡塔琳娜州市镇汇总的次级数据,涵盖2021年至2025年间居住在该州、年龄在5至19岁之间的儿童和青少年。
核心方法
数据来自CIEGES-SC和IBGE 2022年人口普查,计算了各年龄段和各卫生大区的病例发病率。
主要结果
Foram registrados 109.918 (19,0%) casos na faixa etária de 5 a 19 anos, com o ano de 2024 concentrando o maior número de notificações, atingindo uma incidência estadual de 4.396,9 casos por 100/mil/hab. As faixas etárias de 5 a 9 anos (24,7%) e 15 a 17 anos (24,0%) foram as mais acometidas, enquanto as macrorregiões do Planalto Norte e Nordeste e do Vale do Itajaí concentraram, respectivamente, 35,3% e 34,3% dos casos.
解释与创新
研究结果表明,登革热在儿童和青少年中的病例分布具有异质性,时间上集中在2024年,某些卫生大区尤为突出,这表明需要在学校环境中实施针对性的健康教育策略。
科研与公卫意义
登革热病例的空间与时间分布可为学校环境中的流行病学监测和健康教育行动提供信息,有助于在易感人群中预防和控制该疾病。
局限与证据强度
证据基于次级数据和汇总数据,这可能限制结果的精确性和普遍性,但计算出的发病率以及按年龄段和地区的分布与所采用的描述性方法学是一致的。

DISTRIBUIÇÃO ESPACIAL E TEMPORAL DA DENGUE EM CRIANÇAS E ADOLESCENTES EM SANTA CATARINA (2021–2025): SUBSÍDIOS PARA AÇÕES DE EDUCAÇÃO EM SAÚDE NO AMBIENTE ESCOLAR

Authors: Charles Junior Finco, Patrícia Poltronieri, Claudia Cristina Marmentini, Maria Assunta Busato
Original abstract (English)
O ano de 2024 marcou a maior epidemia de dengue já registrada no país, com repercussão expressiva em Santa Catarina. Objetivo: analisar a distribuição espacial e temporal dos casos prováveis de dengue em crianças e adolescentes residentes em Santa Catarina, de 2021 a 2025. Método: estudo descritivo, quantitativo e retrospectivo, com dados secundários e agregados por município, obtidos junto ao CIEGES-SC, referentes à faixa etária escolar (5-19 anos), e dados populacionais do IBGE (Censo 2022). Calculou-se a incidência em relação ao total de casos no Estado, por faixa etária e por macrorregião de saúde. Resultados: foram registrados 109.918 (19,0%) casos na faixa etária de 5 a 19 anos. O ano de 2024 concentrou o maior número de notificações, com incidência estadual de 4.396,9 casos por 100/mil/hab. As faixas de 5 a 9 anos (24,7%) e 15 a 17 anos (24,0%) foram as mais acometidas. As macrorregiões do Planalto Norte e Nordeste e do Vale do Itajaí concentraram, respectivamente, 35,3% e 34,3% dos casos. Conclusão: a dengue acometeu de forma heterogênea crianças e adolescentes catarinenses, com concentração temporal em 2024 e em macrorregiões específicas, o que reforça a necessidade de estratégias de educação em saúde direcionadas ao ambiente escolar.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
O estudo foi conduzido para analisar a distribuição espacial e temporal dos casos prováveis de dengue em crianças e adolescentes, devido à maior epidemia registrada no país em 2024, com impacto significativo em Santa Catarina.
Design and population
Trata-se de um estudo descritivo, quantitativo e retrospectivo, utilizando dados secundários e agregados por município de crianças e adolescentes residentes em Santa Catarina, na faixa etária de 5 a 19 anos, entre 2021 e 2025.
Core methods
Os dados foram obtidos junto ao CIEGES-SC e ao Censo 2022 do IBGE, sendo calculada a incidência dos casos por faixa etária e por macrorregião de saúde.
Main results
Foram registrados 109.918 (19,0%) casos na faixa etária de 5 a 19 anos, com o ano de 2024 concentrando o maior número de notificações, atingindo uma incidência estadual de 4.396,9 casos por 100/mil/hab. As faixas etárias de 5 a 9 anos (24,7%) e 15 a 17 anos (24,0%) foram as mais acometidas, enquanto as macrorregiões do Planalto Norte e Nordeste e do Vale do Itajaí concentraram, respectivamente, 35,3% e 34,3% dos casos.
Interpretation and novelty
Os resultados mostram uma distribuição heterogênea dos casos de dengue entre crianças e adolescentes, com concentração temporal em 2024 e em certas macrorregiões, indicando a necessidade de estratégias de educação em saúde direcionadas ao ambiente escolar.
Scientific and public-health significance
A distribuição espacial e temporal dos casos de dengue pode informar ações de vigilância epidemiológica e educação em saúde no ambiente escolar, contribuindo para a prevenção e controle da doença em populações vulneráveis.
Limitations and evidence strength
A evidência é baseada em dados secundários e agregados, o que pode limitar a precisão e a generalização dos resultados, mas a incidência calculada e a distribuição por faixa etária e região são consistentes com a metodologia descritiva utilizada.

气候变化因素和简单阈值用于越南登革热早期预警:One Health视角

Các yếu tố khí hậu và ngưỡng đơn giản cho cảnh báo sớm sốt xuất huyết Dengue tại Việt Nam: góc nhìn One Health (“Climatic determinants” and simple thresholds for dengue early warning in Vietnam)
作者： Quân K. Nguyễn, Andrew W. Taylor-Robinson, Andrew William Taylor-Robinson
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨气候变化、城市化和水文变化对登革热季节性变化的影响,并指出基于5年平均的预警阈值已不再有效于检测疫情。
设计与对象
该研究是一项基于已发表文献的简要综述,重点探讨气候因素与越南及东南亚地区登革热之间的关系,包括对埃及伊蚊的实验研究。
核心方法
研究从10个气候信号中提取了气候阈值、风险趋势、作用机制和预警时间,同时通过One Health视角评估了三种预测方法(概率超组合、传播途径渠道、机器学习)的性能。
主要结果
研究确定了10个气候阈值,预测时间长达6个月,且存在地区差异。一些信号如干旱、温度振幅大和热浪可能会增加或减少疫情风险,具体取决于实际情况。
解释与创新
作者认为气候信号是预警信号,而非准确预测,建议建立一个三层结合的早期预警系统以提高对疫情的应对能力。
科研与公卫意义
结果可能有助于设计更有效的早期预警系统,根据地区和气候条件调整资源,从而提高在气候变化背景下的登革热疫情控制能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究在应用前需要利用本地监测数据对阈值进行校正,同时预测方法可能在像 COVID-19 大流行这样的重大事件中效果不佳。基于已公布的证据,评估结果为中等水平。

Các yếu tố khí hậu và ngưỡng đơn giản cho cảnh báo sớm sốt xuất huyết Dengue tại Việt Nam: góc nhìn One Health (“Climatic determinants” and simple thresholds for dengue early warning in Vietnam)

Authors: Quân K. Nguyễn, Andrew W. Taylor-Robinson, Andrew William Taylor-Robinson
Original abstract (English)
This mini-review distills ten climatic thresholds (temperature, rainfall, drought, heatwaves, El Niño) with lead times of up to six months that vary by region, and proposes a hybrid One Health early warning system. • Thiết kế: tổng quan ngắn (mini-review) dạng tường thuật. Các yếu tố khí hậu và ngưỡng đơn giản cho cảnh báo sớm sốt xuất huyết Dengue tại Việt Nam: góc nhìn One Health “Climatic determinants” and simple thresholds for dengue early warning in Vietnam: a One Health perspective Thông tin về tác giả: Nguyễn Khởi Quân1,2, Andrew W. Taylor-Robinson1,3 Đơn vị công tác: 1Viện Khoa học Sức khỏe, Trường Đại học VinUni, Hà Nội; 2Johns Hopkins Bloomberg School of Public Health, Hoa Kỳ; 3Center for Global Health, University of Pennsylvania, Hoa Kỳ Thông tin liên hệ: Email: quan.nk@vinuni.edu.vn · Địa chỉ: Trường Đại học VinUni, Vinhomes Ocean Park, Hà Nội · Bài gốc: Science in One Health 2026;5:100159 ĐẶT VẤN ĐỀ / MỤC TIÊU NGHIÊN CỨU ĐỐI TƯỢNG – PHƯƠNG PHÁP NGHIÊN CỨU TÓM TẮT / ABSTRACT KẾT QUẢ KẾT LUẬN & ĐỀ XUẤT TÀI LIỆU THAM KHẢO TRIỂN LÃM POSTER KHOA HỌC DENGUE IN ACTION SỐT XUẤT HUYẾT DENGUE: ỨNG DỤNG KHOA HỌC VÀO THỰC TIỄN HƯỚNG TỚI MỤC TIÊU QUẢN LÝ TOÀN DIỆN HỘI NGHỊ SỐT XUẤT HUYẾT DENGUE NĂM 2026 DENGUE SUMMIT 2026 Sốt xuất huyết Dengue (SXHD) tại Việt Nam đang chuyển từlưu hành theo mùa sang bất ổn do khí hậu chi phối; cảnh báo dựa trên sốca trung bình 5 năm gây báo động giảvà bỏsót dịch. Tổng quan ngắn này đúc kết 10 ngưỡng khí hậu (nhiệt độ, mưa, hạn hán, nắng nóng, El Niño) với thời gian báo trước tới 6 tháng, có giá trịkhác nhau theo vùng, và đềxuất hệthống cảnh báo sớm kết hợp theo tiếp cận Một sức khỏe. Dengue in Vietnam is shifting from predictable seasonality to climate-driven instability, and early warning based on five-year case averages yields false...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nghiên cứu nhằm giải quyết vấn đề về sự thay đổi tính mùa của sốt xuất huyết Dengue do biến đổi khí hậu, đô thị hóa và biến đổi thủy văn, đồng thời chỉ ra rằng các ngưỡng dự báo dựa trên trung bình 5 năm không còn hiệu quả trong việc phát hiện dịch bệnh.
Design and population
Nghiên cứu là một tổng quan ngắn (mini-review) dựa trên các tài liệu đã công bố, tập trung vào mối liên hệ giữa các yếu tố khí hậu và sốt xuất huyết Dengue tại Việt Nam và Đông Nam Á, bao gồm cả các thí nghiệm trên muỗi Aedes.
Core methods
Nghiên cứu đã trích xuất các ngưỡng khí hậu, chiều hướng nguy cơ, cơ chế tác động và thời gian báo trước từ 10 tín hiệu khí hậu, đồng thời đánh giá ba phương pháp dự báo (siêu tổ hợp xác suất, kênh dịch tễ, học máy) thông qua lăng kính One Health.
Main results
Nghiên cứu xác định 10 ngưỡng khí hậu với thời gian báo trước lên đến 6 tháng, có sự khác biệt theo vùng. Một số tín hiệu như hạn hán, biên độ nhiệt lớn và nắng nóng có thể làm tăng hoặc giảm nguy cơ dịch, tùy thuộc vào điều kiện cụ thể.
Interpretation and novelty
Tác giả cho rằng các tín hiệu khí hậu là lời kêu gọi sẵn sàng, không phải là lời dự báo chính xác, và đề xuất một hệ thống cảnh báo sớm kết hợp ba tầng để cải thiện khả năng ứng phó với dịch bệnh.
Scientific and public-health significance
Kết quả có thể hỗ trợ thiết kế hệ thống cảnh báo sớm hiệu quả hơn, điều chỉnh nguồn lực dựa trên vùng và điều kiện khí hậu, từ đó nâng cao khả năng kiểm soát dịch sốt xuất huyết Dengue trong bối cảnh biến đổi khí hậu.
Limitations and evidence strength
Nghiên cứu có hạn chế trong việc cần hiệu chỉnh ngưỡng bằng dữ liệu giám sát địa phương trước khi áp dụng, đồng thời các phương pháp dự báo có thể không hiệu quả trong các biến cố lớn như dịch COVID-19. Dựa trên các bằng chứng đã công bố, đánh giá là ở mức trung bình.
